        foreach (var key in new[] { "MasterVolume", "BGMVolume", "SFXVolume" })
            if (_mixer != null) { float v = PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(key, 1f); _mixer.SetFloat(key, v <= 0.001f ? -80f : Mathf.Log10(v) * 20f); }
    }

    /// <summary>타이틀의 '환경설정' 버튼: 마을 ESC 설정 화면을 그대로 띄운다.</summary>
    public static void OpenSettings()
    {
        if (_instance == null) Create();
        if (_instance == null || _instance._isOpen) return;
        _instance._settingsOnly = true;
        _instance.Open(Style.Village, Page.Settings);
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (_instance != this) return;
        SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnSceneLoaded;
        if (_isOpen) Time.timeScale = _timeScaleBeforeOpen;
        _instance = null;
    }

    private void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
    {
        if (!_isOpen) return;
        _isOpen = false;
        _root.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        Time.timeScale = 1f;
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        var keyboard = Keyboard.current;
        bool escape = keyboard != null && keyboard.escapeKey.isPressed;
        if (!escape) { _escapeHeld = false; return; }
        if (_escapeHeld) return;
        _escapeHeld = true;

        // 확인 팝업이 열려 있으면 ESC는 팝업을 닫는 데만 쓴다.
        if (ModalDialog.BlocksEscape) return;
        // 여정 지도가 떠 있으면 ESC는 지도를 닫는다. (JourneyMap)
        if (JourneyMap.IsOpen) { JourneyMap.Close(); return; }

        if (_isOpen)
        {
            if (_page == Page.Credit) ShowPage(Page.Settings);
            else if (_page == Page.Menu || _settingsOnly) Close();
            else ShowPage(Page.Menu);
            return;
        }

        var style = StyleFor(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name);
        if (style == Style.None) return;
        Open(style);
    }

    private static Style StyleFor(string sceneName) => sceneName switch
    {
        MainScene => Style.Village,
        BattleScene => Style.Battle,
        _ => Style.None,
    };

    private void Open(Style style, Page page = Page.Menu)
    {
        _style = style;
        _timeScaleBeforeOpen = Time.timeScale;
        Time.timeScale = 0f;
        _isOpen = true;
        _root.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        ShowPage(page);
    }

    public void Close()
    {
        if (!_isOpen) return;
        _isOpen = false;
        _root.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        Clear();
        _settingsOnly = false;
        Time.timeScale = _timeScaleBeforeOpen;
    }

    // ───────────────────────────── 화면 구성 ─────────────────────────────

    private void ShowPage(Page page)
    {
        _page = page;
        Clear();
        BuildBackdrop();
        if (!_settingsOnly && page != Page.Credit) BuildGear();
        switch (page)
        {
            case Page.Menu: BuildMenu(); break;
            case Page.ConfirmRetry: BuildConfirmRetry(); break;
            case Page.Settings: BuildSettings(); break;
            case Page.Credit: BuildCredit(); break;
        }
        EventSystem.current?.SetSelectedGameObject(null);
    }

    private void Clear()
    {
        for (int i = _root.childCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) Destroy(_root.GetChild(i).gameObject);
    }

    private void BuildBackdrop()
    {
        var backdrop = NewRect("Backdrop", _root);
        Stretch(backdrop);
        var image = backdrop.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        image.raycastTarget = true; // 메뉴 뒤의 게임 화면 클릭을 막는다.
        if (_style == Style.Village)
        {
            image.sprite = _villageShade;
            image.color = Color.white;
        }
        else
        {
            image.color = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.74f);
        }
    }

    private void BuildGear()
    {
        if (_gear == null) return;
        var rect = NewRect("Gear", _root);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(108f, 108f);
        rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-88f, -75f);
        var image = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        image.sprite = _gear;
        image.preserveAspect = true;
        AddButton(rect.gameObject, image, () => ShowPage(_page == Page.Settings ? Page.Menu : Page.Settings), 0.85f);
    }

    private void BuildMenu()
    {
        var items = new List<(string label, Action action)> { ("계속하기", Close) };
        if (_style == Style.Battle && RunSave.HasCurrentBattle) items.Add(("재도전", () => ShowPage(Page.ConfirmRetry)));
        items.Add(("타이틀화면으로", () => LeaveTo(TitleScene)));
        items.Add(("설정", () => ShowPage(Page.Settings)));
        items.Add(("종료", Quit));

        if (_style == Style.Battle)
        {
            // 전투: 버튼 높이(긴 문구는 더 큼) + 빈칸으로 쌓고, 묶음 전체를 화면 가운데에 둔다. (ESC_조정.png)
            float total = -BattleGap;
            foreach (var item in items) total += BattleHeight(item.label) + BattleGap;
            float y = total * 0.5f + MenuCenterY;
            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                float height = BattleHeight(item.label);
                y -= height * 0.5f;
                MakeMenuButton(item.label, y, item.action);
                y -= height * 0.5f + BattleGap;
            }
            return;
        }

        float firstY = (items.Count - 1) * Spacing * 0.5f + MenuCenterY;
        for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i++)
            MakeMenuButton(items[i].label, firstY - Spacing * i, items[i].action);
    }

    private void BuildConfirmRetry()
    {
        MakeLabel("보스전을 다시 시작할까요?", _bold, 50f, Color.white, new Vector2(ColumnX, 190f), 900f);
        MakeLabel("보스 등장 장면부터 다시 싸워요.\n처치한 보스와 편성은 그대로예요.", _light, 32f,
            new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.8f), new Vector2(ColumnX, 95f), 900f, 100f);
        MakeMenuButton("재도전", -40f, RetryBattle);
        MakeMenuButton("돌아가기", -40f - Spacing, () => ShowPage(Page.Menu));
    }

    private void BuildSettings()
    {
        MakeLabel("설정", _bold, 56f, Color.white, new Vector2(SettingsRowX, 250f), 600f);
        MakeVolumeRow("마스터", "MasterVolume", 120f);
        MakeVolumeRow("배경음", "BGMVolume", 20f);
        MakeVolumeRow("효과음", "SFXVolume", -80f);
        MakeMenuButton("돌아가기", -230f, () => { if (_settingsOnly) Close(); else ShowPage(Page.Menu); });
        BuildCreditLink();
    }

    // 크레딧: 설정 화면 오른쪽 아래 구석의 작고 흐린 글자. 눈에 띄지 않게 숨겨 둔다. (업적 ACH-10)
    private void BuildCreditLink()
    {
        var rect = NewRect("Credit", _root);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 0f);
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(160f, 50f);
        rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-110f, 45f);
        var text = AddText(rect, "Credit", _light, 26f, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.35f));
        text.characterSpacing = 6f;
        AddButton(rect.gameObject, text, () => ShowPage(Page.Credit), 0.92f);
    }

    // 옛 설정창의 크레딧과 같은 화면: 검은 바탕에 엔딩 그림(Resources/Ending)
    private void BuildCredit()
    {
        var panel = NewRect("CreditPanel", _root);
        Stretch(panel);
        var bg = panel.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        bg.color = Color.black;
        var ending = Resources.Load<Texture2D>("Ending");
        if (ending != null)
        {
            var art = NewRect("Ending", panel);
            Stretch(art);
            var image = art.gameObject.AddComponent<RawImage>();
            image.texture = ending;
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            var fit = art.gameObject.AddComponent<AspectRatioFitter>();
            fit.aspectMode = AspectRatioFitter.AspectMode.FitInParent;
            fit.aspectRatio = (float)ending.width / ending.height;
        }
        var back = NewRect("Back", _root);
        back.anchorMin = back.anchorMax = new Vector2(0f, 0f);
        back.sizeDelta = new Vector2(260f, 70f);
        back.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(170f, 70f);
        var label = AddText(back, "돌아가기", _bold, 38f, Color.white);
        AddButton(back.gameObject, label, () => ShowPage(Page.Settings), 0.85f);
        if (AchievementManager.Instance != null) { AchievementManager.Instance.AddProgress("ACH-10", 1); PlayerPrefs.Save(); }
    }

    // 설정 화면의 슬라이더 줄 중심. 마을은 오른쪽 끝에 붙어 있어 안쪽으로 당긴다.
    private float SettingsRowX => _style == Style.Village ? ColumnX - 150f : ColumnX;

    private float Spacing => _style == Style.Village ? VillageSpacing : BattleSpacing;
    private float ColumnX => _style == Style.Village ? VillageColumnX : BattleColumnX;
    private float MenuCenterY => _style == Style.Village ? -40f : 0f;
    private Vector2 ColumnAnchor => _style == Style.Village ? new Vector2(1f, 0.5f) : new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);

    private void MakeMenuButton(string label, float y, Action action)
    {
        if (_style == Style.Village) MakeVillageButton(label, y, action);
        else MakeBattleButton(label, y, action);
    }

    // 마을: 글자만 있는 메뉴 (ESC.png)
    private void MakeVillageButton(string label, float y, Action action)
    {
        var rect = NewRect(label, _root);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = ColumnAnchor;
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(420f, 96f);
        rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(ColumnX, y);
        var text = AddText(rect, label, _bold, 50f, Color.white);
        text.characterSpacing = SpacingFor(label);
        AddButton(rect.gameObject, text, action, 0.92f);
    }

    // 전투: 붓터치 해골 버튼 (ESC2.png)
    private void MakeBattleButton(string label, float y, Action action)
    {
        var rect = NewRect(label, _root);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = ColumnAnchor;
        bool isLong = IsLongLabel(label);
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(isLong ? BattleLongWidth : BattleButtonWidth, BattleButtonHeight);
        rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(ColumnX, y);
        // 마우스 올림 효과(UIHoverScale)가 이 크기를 기준으로 삼도록 AddButton보다 먼저 정한다.
        if (isLong) rect.localScale = new Vector3(BattleLongScale, BattleLongScale, 1f);
        var image = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        if (_brush != null)
        {
            image.sprite = _brush;
            image.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
        }
        else
        {
            image.color = new Color(0.9f, 0.9f, 0.9f, 0.95f);
        }

        var textRect = NewRect("Text", rect);
        Stretch(textRect);
        // 왼쪽 해골을 피해 글자를 놓는다.
        textRect.offsetMin = new Vector2(92f, 0f);
        textRect.offsetMax = new Vector2(-48f, 0f);
        var text = AddText(textRect, label, _bold, 46f, new Color(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.08f));
        text.characterSpacing = SpacingFor(label);
        text.raycastTarget = false;
        // 긴 문구가 해골이나 붓 끝에 닿지 않게 칸에 맞춰 줄인다.
        text.enableAutoSizing = true;
        text.fontSizeMin = 30f;
        text.fontSizeMax = 46f;
        AddButton(rect.gameObject, image, action, 0.96f);
    }

    private static bool IsLongLabel(string label) => label.Length > 4;
    private static float BattleHeight(string label) => BattleButtonHeight * (IsLongLabel(label) ? BattleLongScale : 1f);

    // 예시 이미지처럼 짧은 단어는 글자 사이를 넓게 벌린다.
    private static float SpacingFor(string label) => label.Length switch
    {
        2 => 40f,
        3 => 55f,
        4 => 14f,
        _ => 0f,
    };

    private TextMeshProUGUI MakeLabel(string value, TMP_FontAsset font, float size, Color color, Vector2 pos, float width, float height = 80f)
    {
        var rect = NewRect("Label", _root);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = ColumnAnchor;
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(width, height);
        rect.anchoredPosition = pos;
        var text = AddText(rect, value, font, size, color);
        text.raycastTarget = false;
        return text;
    }

    private void MakeVolumeRow(string label, string key, float y)
    {
        var textColor = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.9f);
        float rowX = SettingsRowX;

        var labelRect = NewRect(label, _root);
        labelRect.anchorMin = labelRect.anchorMax = ColumnAnchor;
        labelRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(150f, 60f);
        labelRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(rowX - 190f, y);
        var text = AddText(labelRect, label, _light, 34f, textColor);
        text.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Right;
        text.raycastTarget = false;

        var slider = MakeSlider(new Vector2(rowX + 60f, y));
        slider.SetValueWithoutNotify(PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(key, 1f));
        slider.onValueChanged.AddListener(value => SetVolume(key, value));
    }

    private Slider MakeSlider(Vector2 pos)
    {
        var rect = NewRect("Slider", _root);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = ColumnAnchor;
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(320f, 40f);
        rect.anchoredPosition = pos;

        var bar = NewRect("Bar", rect);
        bar.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
        bar.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 0.5f);
        bar.sizeDelta = new Vector2(0f, 6f);
        bar.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>().color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.25f);

        var fillArea = NewRect("Fill Area", rect);
        fillArea.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
        fillArea.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 0.5f);
        fillArea.sizeDelta = new Vector2(0f, 6f);
        var fill = NewRect("Fill", fillArea);
        Stretch(fill);
        fill.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>().color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.9f);

        var handleArea = NewRect("Handle Slide Area", rect);
        Stretch(handleArea);
        var handle = NewRect("Handle", handleArea);
        // 슬라이더가 손잡이의 세로를 부모 높이(40)에 맞춰 늘리므로 -10을 주어 30x30 원으로 만든다.
        handle.sizeDelta = new Vector2(30f, -10f);
        var handleImage = handle.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        handleImage.sprite = _knob;

        var slider = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Slider>();
        slider.fillRect = fill;
        slider.handleRect = handle;
        slider.targetGraphic = handleImage;
        slider.direction = Slider.Direction.LeftToRight;
        slider.minValue = 0f;
        slider.maxValue = 1f;
        var nav = slider.navigation;
        nav.mode = Navigation.Mode.None;
        slider.navigation = nav;
        return slider;
    }

    // ───────────────────────────── 동작 ─────────────────────────────

    private void RetryBattle()
    {
        if (SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name != BattleScene || !RunSave.HasCurrentBattle) return;
        // 체크포인트에서 현재 보스전을 등장 장면부터 다시 시작한다. (처치한 보스, 편성, 소울 유지)
        RunSave.RequestResume();
        LeaveTo(BattleScene);
    }

    private void LeaveTo(string sceneName)
    {
        SaveBattleFormation();
        _isOpen = false;
        _root.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        Time.timeScale = 1f;
        SceneManager.LoadScene(sceneName);
    }

    private void Quit()
    {
        SaveBattleFormation();
        PlayerPrefs.Save();
        Close();
#if UNITY_EDITOR
        UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying = false;
#else
        Application.Quit();
#endif
    }

    private static void SaveBattleFormation()
    {
        if (SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name != BattleScene) return;
        var battle = FindFirstObjectByType<BattleDisplayManager>();
        if (battle != null) battle.SaveFormation();
    }

    // SettingManager와 같은 방식으로 믹서에 적용하고 저장한다.
    private void SetVolume(string key, float value)
    {
        if (_mixer != null) _mixer.SetFloat(key, value <= 0.001f ? -80f : Mathf.Log10(value) * 20f);
        PlayerPrefs.SetFloat(key, value);
        PlayerPrefs.Save();
        if (key == "MasterVolume" && value <= 0.001f && AchievementManager.Instance != null)
            AchievementManager.Instance.AddProgress("ACH-27", 1);
    }

    // ───────────────────────────── 공용 ─────────────────────────────

    private static RectTransform NewRect(string name, Transform parent)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform));
        go.layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("UI");
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        return rect;
    }

    private static void Stretch(RectTransform rect)
    {
        rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        rect.offsetMin = rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
    }

    private static TextMeshProUGUI AddText(RectTransform rect, string value, TMP_FontAsset font, float size, Color color)
    {
        var text = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
        if (font != null) text.font = font;
        text.text = value;
        text.fontSize = size;
        text.color = color;
        text.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
        text.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
        return text;
    }

    private static void AddButton(GameObject target, Graphic graphic, Action action, float normalTint)
    {
        var button = target.AddComponent<Button>();
        button.targetGraphic = graphic;
        var colors = button.colors;
        colors.normalColor = new Color(normalTint, normalTint, normalTint, 1f);
        colors.highlightedColor = Color.white;
        colors.selectedColor = new Color(normalTint, normalTint, normalTint, 1f);
        colors.pressedColor = new Color(0.7f, 0.7f, 0.7f, 1f);
        colors.fadeDuration = 0.08f;
        button.colors = colors;
        var nav = button.navigation;
        nav.mode = Navigation.Mode.None;
        button.navigation = nav;
        button.onClick.AddListener(() => action?.Invoke());
        UIHoverScale.Attach(target);
    }

    private static Sprite CreateCircle(int size)
    {
        var texture = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false) { name = "EscKnob" };
        float r = size * 0.5f;
        for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
            {
                float d = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f), new Vector2(r, r));
                texture.SetPixel(x, y, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, Mathf.Clamp01(r - d)));
            }
        texture.Apply();
        return Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, size, size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f));
    }

    // 왼쪽은 그대로 두고 오른쪽으로 갈수록 어두워지는 그라데이션 (ESC.png)
    private static Sprite CreateVillageShade()
    {
        const int width = 256;
        var texture = new Texture2D(width, 1, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false) { wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp, name = "EscVillageShade" };
        for (int x = 0; x < width; x++)
        {
            float t = x / (width - 1f);
            float alpha = Mathf.Lerp(0.08f, 0.94f, Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, Mathf.InverseLerp(0.45f, 0.82f, t)));
            texture.SetPixel(x, 0, new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, alpha));
        }
        texture.Apply();
        return Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, width, 1), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f));
    }
}

using NUnit.Framework;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

public class GameManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    private GameObject Hitted_Object;
    private GameObject PlayerPrefeb;
    private GameObject BossPrefeb;

    public static Vector2[,] position = new Vector2[9, 9];

    private GameObject MainBoard;
    private float MainX;
    private float MainY;

    private float LastClickTime = 0f;
    private float SceneTime;

    private bool IsDoubleClicked;
    private bool isSelected = false;
    private bool isMoving;

    private Vector3 targetPosition;

    public float selectedScaleMultiplier = 1.2f;
    public float moveSpeed = 10f;

    private string[] Player_Names = { "Kight", "Archer", "Thief" };
    private int[] Player_Atk = { 6, 4, 3 };
    private int[] Player_HH = { 1, 3, 4 };

    private string[] Item_Name = { "Old_Sword" };

    private string[] Boss_Names = { "BigHouse" };
    private int[] Boss_Hp = { 100 };
    private int[] Boss_Atk = { 5 };

    private int bossindex;

    private Player[] players = new Player[3];

    private Turn tn;
    private Player pl;
    private Plate pt;
    private Items it;

    // 현재 선택된 Player
    private Player selectedPlayer;


    static public int PlayerHP;
    static public int BossHP;


    // 보드의 좌표 범위
    public static float BoardMinX;
    public static float BoardMaxX;
    public static float BoardMinY;
    public static float BoardMaxY;


    public static float[] player_x = new float[3];
    public static int[] player_board_x = new int[3];

    public static float[] player_y = new float[3];
    public static int[] player_board_y = new int[3];


    public int BossIndex
    {
        get
        {
            return bossindex;
        }

        set
        {
            switch (bossindex)
            {
                case 0:

                    break;
            }
        }
    }

    private void Awake()
    {
        // 통합 전투는 Canvas/UI 입력을 사용한다. 월드 Raycast 컨트롤러가 같이 실행되면
        // 같은 클릭과 HP를 이중으로 처리하므로 UI 전투 씬에서는 이 프로토타입을 끈다.
        if (FindAnyObjectByType<BoardPanel>() != null)
        {
            enabled = false;
        }
    }


    void Start()
    {
        tn = FindAnyObjectByType<Turn>();
        pl = FindAnyObjectByType<Player>();
        pt = FindAnyObjectByType<Plate>();
        it = FindAnyObjectByType<Items>();

        players[0] = GameObject.Find("Player1").GetComponent<Player>();
        players[1] = GameObject.Find("Player2").GetComponent<Player>();
        players[2] = GameObject.Find("Player3").GetComponent<Player>();


        players[0].CharacterIndex = 1;
        players[1].CharacterIndex = 2;
        players[2].CharacterIndex = 3;


        PositionSetting();


        PositionMove(0, 3, 3);
        PositionMove(1, 4, 4);
        PositionMove(2, 5, 5);


        Activate_Player(0, 0);
        Activate_Player(1, 1);
        Activate_Player(2, 2);


        Activate_Boss(0);

        it.Activate_Items(0, 0);

        PlayerHP = 15;
    }


    void Update()
    {
        SceneTime = Time.time;


        // 마우스 왼쪽 클릭
        if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0))
        {
            Vector2 mousePos =
                Camera.main.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);


            RaycastHit2D hit =
                Physics2D.Raycast(mousePos, Vector2.zero);


            // 아무것도 맞지 않았다면 종료
            if (hit.collider == null)
            {
                return;
            }


            Hitted_Object = hit.collider.gameObject;


            if (Hitted_Object.name.Contains("Item"))
            {
                int ItemIndex = int.Parse(Hitted_Object.name.Replace("Item", ""));
                Debug.Log(ItemIndex);
                it.Use_Item(it.Public_ItemNumber[ItemIndex-1]);
            }   

            // 턴이 남아있을 때만 행동
            if (Turn.TurnCount > 0)
            {
                // =========================================
                // Player 클릭
                // =========================================
                if (Hitted_Object.name.Contains("Player"))
                {
                    // 실제로 클릭한 Player를 가져온다
                    selectedPlayer =
                        Hitted_Object.GetComponent<Player>();


                    // Player 컴포넌트가 없다면 종료
                    if (selectedPlayer == null)
                    {
                        return;
                    }


                    // Plate에게 현재 선택한 Player를 알려준다
                    pt.Check_Character(selectedPlayer);


                    // =========================================
                    // 더블클릭
                    // =========================================
                    if (SceneTime - LastClickTime <= 0.3f)
                    {
                        IsDoubleClicked = true;

                        LastClickTime = 0f;


                        // 실제 클릭한 Player가 공격
                        selectedPlayer.DoubleClick();


                        // 이동 가능 칸 제거
                        pt.Remove_MovingPlate();
                    }


                    // =========================================
                    // 첫 번째 클릭
                    // =========================================
                    else
                    {
                        IsDoubleClicked = false;

                        LastClickTime = SceneTime;


                        isSelected = true;


                        // 실제 클릭한 Player 확대
                        selectedPlayer.transform.localScale =
                            selectedPlayer.transform.localScale
                            * selectedScaleMultiplier;


                        // 기존 이동 가능 칸 제거
                        pt.Remove_MovingPlate();


                        // 새로운 이동 가능 칸 생성
                        pt.Create_MovingPlate(7);
                    }
                }


                // =========================================
                // MovingPoint 클릭
                // =========================================
                else if (isSelected &&
                         hit.collider.CompareTag("MovePoint"))
                {
                    // 이동할 위치 저장
                    targetPosition =
                        hit.collider.transform.position;


                    // 이동 시작
                    isMoving = true;


                    // 선택 해제
                    isSelected = false;


                    // Player 크기 원래대로
                    selectedPlayer.transform.localScale =
                        selectedPlayer.transform.localScale
                        / selectedScaleMultiplier;


                    // 이동 가능 칸 제거
                    pt.Remove_MovingPlate();


                    // Player 방향 전환
                    selectedPlayer.FlipX();
                }
            }
        }

        if (isMoving && selectedPlayer != null)
        {
            selectedPlayer.transform.position =
                Vector3.MoveTowards(
                    selectedPlayer.transform.position,
                    targetPosition,
                    moveSpeed * Time.deltaTime
                );


            if (Vector3.Distance(
                    selectedPlayer.transform.position,
                    targetPosition) < 0.01f)
            {
                // 정확히 목표 위치로 이동
                selectedPlayer.transform.position =
                    targetPosition;


                isMoving = false;


                // =========================================
                // 현재 Player의 Unity 좌표 저장
                // =========================================

                player_x[selectedPlayer.CharacterIndex - 1] =
                    targetPosition.x;

                player_y[selectedPlayer.CharacterIndex - 1] =
                    targetPosition.y;


                // =========================================
                // Unity 좌표 → Board 좌표
                // =========================================

                player_board_x[selectedPlayer.CharacterIndex - 1] =
                    Change_Coordinate_X_To_Board_X();

                player_board_y[selectedPlayer.CharacterIndex - 1] =
                    Change_Coordinate_Y_To_Board_Y();


                Debug.Log(
                    Change_Coordinate_X_To_Board_X()
                    + " , "
                    + Change_Coordinate_Y_To_Board_Y()
                );


                // 턴 1 감소
                tn.TurnCount_Subtract(1);
            }
        }
    }




    // =========================================
    // Player 초기 위치 설정
    // =========================================

    private void PositionMove(
        int CharacterIndex,
        int x,
        int y)
    {
        players[CharacterIndex].transform.position =
            position[x, y];


        player_board_x[CharacterIndex] = x;
        player_board_y[CharacterIndex] = y;
    }


    // =========================================
    // Board 좌표 설정
    // =========================================

    private void PositionSetting()
    {
        MainBoard =
            GameObject.Find("(0, 0)Board");


        MainX =
            MainBoard.transform.position.x;


        MainY =
            MainBoard.transform.position.y;


        for (int y = 0; y < 9; y++)
        {
            for (int x = 0; x < 9; x++)
            {
                position[x, y] =
                    new Vector2(
                        MainX + x,
                        MainY + y
                    );
            }
        }


        BoardMinX = MainX;
        BoardMaxX = MainX + 8;

        BoardMinY = MainY;
        BoardMaxY = MainY + 8;
    }


    // =========================================
    // Player 캐릭터 능력치 설정
    // =========================================

    private void Activate_Player(
        int SelectedNumber,
        int CharacterIndex)
    {
        players[SelectedNumber].CharacterName =
            Player_Names[CharacterIndex];


        players[SelectedNumber].Attck =
            Player_Atk[CharacterIndex];


        players[SelectedNumber].HHh =
            Player_HH[CharacterIndex];
    }


    // =========================================
    // Boss 설정
    // =========================================

    private void Activate_Boss(int BossIndex)
    {
        string name =
            Boss_Names[BossIndex];


        int hp =
            Boss_Hp[BossIndex];


        int atk =
            Boss_Atk[BossIndex];


        BossHP = hp;


        Debug.Log(
            "보스 체력 : " + hp
        );


        bossindex =
            BossIndex;
    }


    // =========================================
    // X 좌표 → Board X 좌표
    // =========================================

    public int Change_Coordinate_X_To_Board_X()
    {
        MainBoard =
            GameObject.Find("(0, 0)Board");


        MainX =
            MainBoard.transform.position.x;


        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++)
        {
            if (Mathf.Approximately(
                    MainX + i,
                    player_x[selectedPlayer.CharacterIndex - 1]))
            {
                return i;
            }
        }


        return -1;
    }


    // =========================================
    // Y 좌표 → Board Y 좌표
    // =========================================

    public int Change_Coordinate_Y_To_Board_Y()
    {
        MainBoard =
            GameObject.Find("(0, 0)Board");


        MainY =
            MainBoard.transform.position.y;


        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++)
        {
            if (Mathf.Approximately(
                    MainY + i,
                    player_y[selectedPlayer.CharacterIndex - 1]))
            {
                return i;
            }
        }


        return -1;
    }


    // =========================================
    // 모든 Player 공격
    // =========================================

    public void Player_All_Attck()
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        {
            BossHP -=
                players[i].Attck;
        }
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/GameSfx.cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

// Observe UI input before scene-changing button callbacks, without replacing them.
[DefaultExecutionOrder(-1000)]
public sealed class GameSfx : MonoBehaviour
{
    private static GameSfx instance;
    private GameSfxSettings settings;
    private AudioSource source;
    private readonly List<RaycastResult> hits = new List<RaycastResult>();
    private readonly Dictionary<AudioClip, int> lastPlayedFrames = new Dictionary<AudioClip, int>();

    [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
    private static void ResetStatics() => instance = null;

    [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.BeforeSceneLoad)]
    private static void Initialize()
    {
        if (instance != null) return;
        new GameObject("Game SFX").AddComponent<GameSfx>();
    }

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (instance != null && instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        instance = this;
        DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);
        settings = Resources.Load<GameSfxSettings>("GameSfxSettings");
        if (settings == null)
        {
            Debug.LogWarning("GameSfxSettings is missing from Resources.");
            enabled = false;
            return;
        }
        source = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
        source.playOnAwake = false;
        source.spatialBlend = 0f;
        source.outputAudioMixerGroup = settings.output;
    }

    private void Start()
    {
        if (settings == null || settings.output == null) return;
        foreach (string parameter in new[] { "MasterVolume", "SFXVolume" })
        {
            float value = PlayerPrefs.GetFloat(parameter, 1f);
            settings.output.audioMixer.SetFloat(parameter, value <= 0.001f ? -80f : Mathf.Log10(value) * 20f);
        }
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        var events = EventSystem.current;
        if (events == null || settings == null) return;
        if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0))
        {
            hits.Clear();
            events.RaycastAll(new PointerEventData(events) { position = Input.mousePosition }, hits);
            // Only the frontmost hit can receive the pointer event.
            if (hits.Count > 0 && IsClickable(hits[0].gameObject)) Play(settings.click);
        }
        if (Input.GetButtonDown("Submit") && events.currentSelectedGameObject != null)
        {
            var button = events.currentSelectedGameObject.GetComponent<Button>();
            if (button != null && button.IsActive() && button.IsInteractable()) Play(settings.click);
        }
    }

    private static bool IsClickable(GameObject target)
    {
        for (Transform node = target.transform; node != null; node = node.parent)
        {
            var selectable = node.GetComponent<Selectable>();
            if (selectable != null) return selectable.IsActive() && selectable.IsInteractable();
            var trigger = node.GetComponent<EventTrigger>();
            if (trigger != null && trigger.isActiveAndEnabled)
            {
                foreach (var entry in trigger.triggers)
                    if (entry.eventID == EventTriggerType.PointerClick || entry.eventID == EventTriggerType.PointerDown)
                        return true;
                // EventTrigger consumes events instead of bubbling to its parents.
                return false;
            }
            if (node.GetComponent<IPointerClickHandler>() != null || node.GetComponent<IPointerDownHandler>() != null)
                return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    private void Play(AudioClip clip)
    {
        if (source == null || clip == null) return;
        // Simultaneous unit attacks share one sound, avoiding multiplied volume.
        if (lastPlayedFrames.TryGetValue(clip, out int frame) && frame == Time.frameCount) return;
        lastPlayedFrames[clip] = Time.frameCount;
        source.PlayOneShot(clip, settings.volume);
    }

    public static void PlayMove() { if (instance != null && instance.settings != null) instance.Play(instance.settings.move); }
    // Extra layers for the next judgment sound (played at the cut-in impact).
    public static bool NextJudgmentBig, NextJudgmentTrait;
    public static void PlayJudgment()
    {
        if (instance == null || instance.settings == null) return;
        instance.Play(instance.settings.judgment);
        if (NextJudgmentBig) instance.Play(instance.settings.bigJudgment);
        if (NextJudgmentTrait) instance.Play(instance.settings.trait);
        NextJudgmentBig = NextJudgmentTrait = false;
    }
    private static void PlayWith(System.Func<GameSfxSettings, AudioClip> pick) { if (instance != null && instance.settings != null) instance.Play(pick(instance.settings)); }
    public static void PlayPickup() => PlayWith(s => s.pickup);
    public static void PlayGreedPickup() => PlayWith(s => s.greedPickup);
    public static void PlayGreedStand() => PlayWith(s => s.greedStand);
    public static void PlayHit() => PlayWith(s => s.hit);
    public static void PlayDown() => PlayWith(s => s.down);
    public static void PlayTrait() => PlayWith(s => s.trait);
    public static void PlayDenied() => PlayWith(s => s.denied);
    public static void PlayPurchase() => PlayWith(s => s.purchase);
    public static void PlayAttack() { if (instance != null && instance.settings != null) instance.Play(instance.settings.attack); }
}

using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Audio;

[CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Audio/Game SFX Settings")]
public sealed class GameSfxSettings : ScriptableObject
{
    public AudioMixerGroup output;
    public AudioClip click;
    public AudioClip move;
    public AudioClip judgment;
    public AudioClip attack;
    [Header("Battle")]
    public AudioClip pickup;
    public AudioClip greedPickup;
    public AudioClip greedStand;
    public AudioClip hit;
    public AudioClip down;
    public AudioClip bigJudgment;
    public AudioClip trait;
    public AudioClip denied;
    [Header("Shop")]
    public AudioClip purchase;
    [Range(0f, 1f)] public float volume = 0.7f;
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/HitShake.cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

// 맞았을 때 흔들림 + 붉은 번쩍임. RectTransform은 건드리지 않고 그리는 정점만 옮긴다.
// (보스 그림처럼 다른 코드가 위치·크기를 쓰는 그래픽에도 안전하게 붙일 수 있다.)
[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public sealed class HitShake : BaseMeshEffect
{
    private static readonly Color FlashColor = new Color(1f, 0.55f, 0.5f, 1f);

    private float _time = -1f;
    private float _duration;
    private float _amplitude;
    private Vector2 _offset;
    private float _flash;

    public static HitShake For(Graphic graphic)
    {
        if (graphic == null) return null;
        var shake = graphic.GetComponent<HitShake>();
        if (shake == null) shake = graphic.gameObject.AddComponent<HitShake>();
        return shake;
    }

    /// <summary>amplitude: 흔들리는 거리(px), duration: 길이(초)</summary>
    public void Play(float amplitude = 14f, float duration = 0.3f)
    {
        _amplitude = amplitude;
        _duration = Mathf.Max(0.01f, duration);
        _time = 0f;
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (_time < 0f) return;
        _time += Time.deltaTime;
        float t = _time / _duration;
        if (t >= 1f)
        {
            _time = -1f;
            _offset = Vector2.zero;
            _flash = 0f;
        }
        else
        {
            float fall = (1f - t) * (1f - t);
            // 처음 한 번은 크게 밀리고(화살이 박히는 방향), 이후 좌우로 잦아든다.
            _offset = new Vector2(Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI * 9f) * _amplitude * fall, Mathf.Cos(t * Mathf.PI * 7f) * _amplitude * 0.25f * fall);
            _flash = Mathf.Clamp01(1f - t * 2.5f);
        }
        if (graphic != null) graphic.SetVerticesDirty();
    }

    protected override void OnDisable()
    {
        _time = -1f;
        _offset = Vector2.zero;
        _flash = 0f;
        base.OnDisable();
    }

    public override void ModifyMesh(VertexHelper vh)
    {
        if (!IsActive() || (_offset == Vector2.zero && _flash <= 0f)) return;
        var v = new UIVertex();
        var tint = Color.Lerp(Color.white, FlashColor, _flash);
        for (int i = 0; i < vh.currentVertCount; i++)
        {
            vh.PopulateUIVertex(ref v, i);
            v.position += (Vector3)_offset;
            Color c = v.color;
            v.color = c * tint;
            vh.SetUIVertex(v, i);
        }
    }
}
