        Heavy(6, 1, "Scavenger_Insect", flight: 0.32f, arc: 120f, spin: 600f),
        // 스캐빈저 보스(영생귀족): 지팡이로 바닥을 치고 금화 세 닢 튕기기
        Light(6, 2, "Scavenger_Boss", count: 3, spin: 1400f),
        // 마법사: 지팡이 휘두르기 → 별 마법탄
        Magic(7, 0, "Wizard", spin: 720f),
        // 마법사 곤충: 더듬이에 번개를 모아 지그재그 번개
        Heavy(7, 1, "Wizard_Insect", flight: 0.1f),
        // 마법사 보스(문): 몸(문)이 열리고 안쪽 눈에서 빛
        Magic(7, 2, "Wizard_Boss"),
        // 광신도: 가슴 문양이 빛나며 떨어져 날아감
        Magic(8, 0, "Fanatic", spin: 240f),
        // 광신도 곤충: 등껍질을 펼쳐 문양 새긴 조각 세 개
        Light(8, 1, "Fanatic_Insect", count: 3, spin: 900f),
        // 광신도 보스(왕): 손가락을 튕기면 왕의 인장이 날아가 찍힘
        Heavy(8, 2, "Fanatic_Boss", flight: 0.26f, arc: 60f),
        // 꼬마 사신: 낫을 크게 돌려 베기 → 검은 반달 참격 (가장 크게)
        Heavy(9, 0, "Reaper", flight: 0.24f, width: 1.6f, shake: 28f),
    };

    // 새 연출 틀. 프레임 그림은 정리 도구가 같은 크기·같은 발 위치로 맞춰 주므로 canvasScale·launchOffset은 공통값에서 시작해 그림을 보고 조정한다.
    private static Profile Std(int unit, int skin, string folder, float[] times)
    {
        string key = folder.ToLowerInvariant();
        return new Profile
        {
            unitId = unit, skin = skin, folder = folder, framePrefix = key + "_act", frameTimes = times, releaseFrame = 3,
            projectile = key + "_shot", canvasScale = 1.4f, launchOffset = new Vector2(0.3f, 0.05f),
            // 컷인 10프레임 약 1.6초: 대기 → 준비 2 → 힘 모음 2 → 최대(멈춤) → 번뜩 → 발사(번쩍) → 여운 → 복귀
            cutPrefix = key + "_cut", cutRelease = 7,
            cutTimes = new[] { 0.13f, 0.11f, 0.12f, 0.11f, 0.12f, 0.28f, 0.20f, 0.08f, 0.22f, 0.25f },
        };
    }

    // 가볍고 빠름: 던지기·쏘기. 박힌 채 머묾.
    private static Profile Light(int unit, int skin, string folder, int count = 1, float spin = 0f, float flight = 0.16f)
    {
        var p = Std(unit, skin, folder, new[] { 0.05f, 0.07f, 0.16f, 0.04f, 0.08f, 0.10f });
        p.projectileWidth = 0.8f; p.flightTime = flight; p.arcHeight = 25f; p.stick = true;
        p.shakeAmplitude = 11f; p.shakeDuration = 0.22f; p.spin = spin;
        p.projectileCount = count; if (count > 1) p.projectileSpread = new Vector2(0f, -0.14f);
        return p;
    }

    // 마법: 모으는 시간이 길고 둥글게 날아가 퍼지며 사라짐.
    private static Profile Magic(int unit, int skin, string folder, float spin = 0f)
    {
        var p = Std(unit, skin, folder, new[] { 0.06f, 0.08f, 0.24f, 0.04f, 0.10f, 0.10f });
        p.projectileWidth = 0.9f; p.flightTime = 0.28f; p.arcHeight = 60f; p.stick = false;
        p.shakeAmplitude = 16f; p.shakeDuration = 0.3f; p.spin = spin;
        return p;
    }

    // 무거운 한 방: 크게 흔들림.
    private static Profile Heavy(int unit, int skin, string folder, float flight = 0.22f, float arc = 0f, bool stick = false, float spin = 0f, float width = 1.3f, float shake = 22f)
    {
        var p = Std(unit, skin, folder, new[] { 0.07f, 0.09f, 0.22f, 0.04f, 0.12f, 0.12f });
        p.projectileWidth = width; p.flightTime = flight; p.arcHeight = arc; p.stick = stick; p.spin = spin;
        p.shakeAmplitude = shake; p.shakeDuration = 0.4f;
        return p;
    }

    private const float StuckTime = 0.12f;
    private const float FadeTime = 0.15f;

    private static readonly Dictionary<Profile, Sprite[]> _frames = new Dictionary<Profile, Sprite[]>();
    private static readonly Dictionary<Profile, Sprite> _projectiles = new Dictionary<Profile, Sprite>();
    private static readonly Dictionary<Profile, Sprite[]> _cutFrames = new Dictionary<Profile, Sprite[]>();

    /// <summary>컷인 그림. 없으면 null.</summary>
    public static Sprite[] CutFrames(Profile p)
    {
        if (p == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(p.cutPrefix) || p.cutTimes == null) return null;
        if (_cutFrames.TryGetValue(p, out var cached)) return cached;
        var frames = new Sprite[p.cutTimes.Length];
        for (int i = 0; i < frames.Length; i++)
            frames[i] = Resources.Load<Sprite>($"Anim/{p.folder}/Cut/{p.cutPrefix}_{i + 1}");
        bool ok = Array.TrueForAll(frames, f => f != null);
        if (!ok) Debug.LogWarning($"JudgeShot: Resources/Anim/{p.folder}/Cut 그림이 모자라서 {p.cutPrefix} 컷인을 건너뜀");
        return _cutFrames[p] = ok ? frames : null;
    }
    private static readonly List<GameObject> _live = new List<GameObject>();

    /// <summary>이 캐릭터·스킨에 심판 연출이 있으면 돌려준다. 그림이 하나라도 없으면 null. (skin: 0 기본, 1 곤충, 2 보스)</summary>
    public static Profile Find(int unitId, int skin = 0)
    {
        foreach (var p in Profiles)
            if (p.unitId == unitId && p.skin == skin) return Load(p) ? p : null;
        return null;
    }

    private static bool Load(Profile p)
    {
        if (_frames.TryGetValue(p, out var cached)) return cached != null;
        var frames = new Sprite[p.frameTimes.Length];
        for (int i = 0; i < frames.Length; i++)
            frames[i] = Resources.Load<Sprite>($"Anim/{p.folder}/{p.framePrefix}_{i + 1}");
        var projectile = Resources.Load<Sprite>($"Anim/{p.folder}/{p.projectile}");
        bool ok = Array.TrueForAll(frames, f => f != null) && projectile != null;
        _frames[p] = ok ? frames : null;
        _projectiles[p] = projectile;
        // 아직 그림을 하나도 넣지 않은 연출은 조용히 건너뛴다. 일부만 있으면 알려 준다.
        if (!ok && (frames[0] != null || projectile != null)) Debug.LogWarning($"JudgeShot: Resources/Anim/{p.folder} 그림이 모자라서 {p.framePrefix} 연출을 건너뜀");
        return ok;
    }

    /// <summary>전투 화면이 꺼질 때 날아가던 것을 지운다.</summary>
    public static void Cleanup()
    {
        foreach (var go in _live) if (go != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(go);
        _live.Clear();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// unit 그림을 프레임으로 바꿔 재생하고, 놓는 순간 target까지 날린다.
    /// 닿으면 onHit, 동작이 끝나면 onDone. 끝나면 원래 그림·크기로 되돌린다.
    /// </summary>
    public static IEnumerator Play(MonoBehaviour host, Profile p, Image unit, RectTransform target,
        Action onRelease, Action<int> onHit, Action onDone)
    {
        var frames = _frames[p];
        var original = unit.sprite;
        var rect = unit.rectTransform;
        var baseSize = rect.sizeDelta;
        // 프레임 그림이 원래보다 넓으면(검이 칸 밖으로 나가므로) 그만큼 키워서 원래 캐릭터 크기와 맞춘다.
        if (!Mathf.Approximately(p.canvasScale, 1f)) rect.sizeDelta = baseSize * p.canvasScale;
        try
        {
            for (int f = 0; f < frames.Length; f++)
            {
                unit.sprite = frames[f];
                if (f == p.releaseFrame)
                {
                    onRelease?.Invoke();
                    for (int j = 0; j < Mathf.Max(1, p.projectileCount); j++)
                    {
                        if (target != null) host.StartCoroutine(Fly(p, unit, baseSize, target, j, onHit));
                        else onHit?.Invoke(j);
                    }
                }
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(p.frameTimes[f]);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            unit.sprite = original;
            rect.sizeDelta = baseSize;
            onDone?.Invoke();
        }
    }

    /// <param name="index">몇 번째로 날아가는지 (0부터). 두 번째부터는 조금 늦게 출발한다.</param>
    private static IEnumerator Fly(Profile p, Image unit, Vector2 unitSize, RectTransform target, int index, Action<int> onHit)
    {
        if (index > 0) yield return new WaitForSeconds(p.projectileInterval * index);
        var canvas = unit != null && unit.canvas != null ? unit.canvas.rootCanvas : null;
        if (canvas == null) { onHit?.Invoke(index); yield break; }
        var root = (RectTransform)canvas.transform;
        var sprite = _projectiles[p];

        var go = new GameObject(p.projectile, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
        go.layer = unit.gameObject.layer;
        _live.RemoveAll(x => x == null);
        _live.Add(go);
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(root, false);
        rect.SetAsLastSibling();
        var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
        image.sprite = sprite;
        image.raycastTarget = false;
        // 화살은 촉 끝이 위치점(박힌 자리), 검기는 가운데가 위치점
        // 도는 것은 가운데를 중심으로 돌아야 자연스럽다
        rect.pivot = p.stick && p.spin == 0f ? new Vector2(1f, 0.5f) : new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
        float width = unitSize.x * p.projectileWidth;
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(width, width * sprite.rect.height / sprite.rect.width);

        var unitRect = unit.rectTransform;
        Vector2 start = ToRoot(root, unitRect, unitRect.rect.center + Vector2.Scale(unitSize, p.launchOffset + p.projectileSpread * index));
        Vector2 end = ToRoot(root, target, target.rect.center + new Vector2(-target.rect.width * 0.12f, target.rect.height * (0.05f - 0.04f * index)));
        float arc = Mathf.Min(p.arcHeight, Vector2.Distance(start, end) * 0.08f);

        Vector2 prev = start;
        rect.anchoredPosition = start;
        rect.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, Mathf.Atan2(end.y - start.y, end.x - start.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
        for (float t = 0f; t < p.flightTime; t += Time.deltaTime)
        {
            if (go == null) yield break;
            float k = t / p.flightTime;
            Vector2 pos = Vector2.Lerp(start, end, k) + Vector2.up * (4f * arc * k * (1f - k));
            rect.anchoredPosition = pos;
            Vector2 dir = pos - prev;
            if (p.spin != 0f) rect.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, -p.spin * t);
            else if (dir.sqrMagnitude > 0.01f) rect.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, Mathf.Atan2(dir.y, dir.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
            prev = pos;
            yield return null;
        }
        if (go == null) yield break;
        rect.anchoredPosition = end;
        // 돌던 것은 박히는 순간 날아온 방향으로 꽂힌다
        if (p.spin != 0f) rect.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, Mathf.Atan2(end.y - start.y, end.x - start.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);

        onHit?.Invoke(index);

        if (p.stick) yield return new WaitForSeconds(StuckTime);
        var size = rect.sizeDelta;
        for (float t = 0f; t < FadeTime && go != null; t += Time.deltaTime)
        {
            float k = t / FadeTime;
            var c = image.color;
            c.a = 1f - k;
            image.color = c;
            if (!p.stick) rect.sizeDelta = size * (1f + 0.4f * k); // 검기는 부딪히며 퍼진다
            yield return null;
        }
        if (go != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(go);
    }

    private static Vector2 ToRoot(RectTransform root, RectTransform from, Vector2 localPoint)
    {
        Vector3 local = root.InverseTransformPoint(from.TransformPoint(localPoint));
        return new Vector2(local.x, local.y) - root.rect.center;
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/LoadingScreen.cs
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;

// 로딩 화면: 오프닝 장면 한 장(랜덤)을 어둡게 깔고, 오른쪽 아래에 "Loading..."(점 1→2→3 반복)과 로딩 바.
// LoadingScreen.Load("Item") 처럼 쓴다. 너무 빨리 끝나도 깜빡이지 않게 최소 시간만큼은 보여 준다.
public sealed class LoadingScreen : MonoBehaviour
{
    private const int SortingOrder = 29000;        // 장면 전환 페이드(30000) 바로 아래, 나머지 UI 위
    private const string OpeningPath = "Images/Opening";
    private static readonly HashSet<string> Excluded = new HashSet<string> { "11" }; // 타이틀과 같은 제외 목록
    private const float FadeIn = 0.25f;
    private const float FadeOut = 0.35f;
    private const float MinimumShow = 2.5f;        // 최소 표시 시간 (이미 불러왔어도 이만큼은 보여 준다)
    private const float DotInterval = 0.4f;        // 점이 하나 늘어나는 간격
    private const float BarWidth = 420f;
    private const float BarHeight = 34f;

    private static LoadingScreen _instance;
    private CanvasGroup _group;
    private RectTransform _fill;
    private TextMeshProUGUI _label;
    private RectTransform _photo;

    public static bool IsLoading => _instance != null;

    public static void Load(string sceneName)
    {
        if (_instance != null || SceneFade.IsFading) return;
        var go = new GameObject("Loading Screen", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Canvas), typeof(CanvasScaler), typeof(GraphicRaycaster), typeof(CanvasGroup));
        DontDestroyOnLoad(go);
        _instance = go.AddComponent<LoadingScreen>();
        _instance.Build();
        _instance.StartCoroutine(_instance.Run(sceneName));
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (_instance == this) _instance = null;
    }

    private void Build()
    {
        var canvas = GetComponent<Canvas>();
        canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
        canvas.sortingOrder = SortingOrder;
        var scaler = GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
        scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
        scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1920f, 1080f);
        scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = 0.5f;
        _group = GetComponent<CanvasGroup>();
        _group.alpha = 0f;
        _group.blocksRaycasts = true; // 로딩 중에는 아무것도 눌리지 않게

        var back = NewImage("Back", transform, Color.black);
        back.raycastTarget = true;
        Stretch(back.rectTransform);

        // 오프닝 장면 한 장 (화면을 꽉 채우고 아주 천천히 확대)
        var photo = NewImage("Photo", transform, Color.white);
        photo.sprite = PickBackground();
        photo.enabled = photo.sprite != null;
        var fitter = photo.gameObject.AddComponent<AspectRatioFitter>();
        fitter.aspectMode = AspectRatioFitter.AspectMode.EnvelopeParent;
        if (photo.sprite != null) fitter.aspectRatio = photo.sprite.rect.width / photo.sprite.rect.height;
        _photo = photo.rectTransform;

        // 글자와 바가 잘 보이게 전체를 어둡게 (리니어 색 공간이라 수치보다 옅게 보여서 높게 잡음)
        var shade = NewImage("Shade", transform, new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.72f));
        Stretch(shade.rectTransform);

        var font = Resources.Load<TMP_FontAsset>("Fonts & Materials/BookkMyungjo_Bold SDF");

        // 로딩 바: 흰 테두리 + 안쪽 흰 채움
        var bar = NewRect("Bar", transform);
        bar.anchorMin = bar.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 0f);
        bar.pivot = new Vector2(1f, 0f);
        bar.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-70f, 60f);
        bar.sizeDelta = new Vector2(BarWidth, BarHeight);
        var frame = NewImage("Frame", bar, new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.35f));
        Stretch(frame.rectTransform);
        var outline = frame.gameObject.AddComponent<Outline>();
        outline.effectColor = Color.white;
        outline.effectDistance = new Vector2(3f, -3f);
        var outline2 = frame.gameObject.AddComponent<Outline>();
        outline2.effectColor = Color.white;
        outline2.effectDistance = new Vector2(-3f, 3f);
        var fill = NewImage("Fill", bar, Color.white);
        _fill = fill.rectTransform;
        _fill.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 0f);
        _fill.anchorMax = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
        _fill.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
        _fill.offsetMin = new Vector2(6f, 6f);
        _fill.offsetMax = new Vector2(6f, -6f);

        // "Loading..." : 바 왼쪽 위
        _label = new GameObject("Label", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(TextMeshProUGUI)).GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
        var lr = _label.rectTransform;
        lr.SetParent(bar, false);
        lr.anchorMin = lr.anchorMax = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
        lr.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 0f);
        lr.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 12f);
        lr.sizeDelta = new Vector2(300f, 50f);
        if (font != null) _label.font = font;
        _label.fontSize = 34f;
        _label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.BottomLeft;
        _label.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
        _label.color = Color.white;
        _label.raycastTarget = false;
        _label.text = "Loading.";
        SetProgress(0f);
    }

    private IEnumerator Run(string sceneName)
    {
        float start = Time.unscaledTime;
        float shown = 0f;

        // 화면을 덮는다
        for (float t = 0f; t < FadeIn; t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime)
        {
            _group.alpha = t / FadeIn;
            Animate(start);
            yield return null;
        }
        _group.alpha = 1f;

        var op = SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(sceneName);
        if (op == null) { Destroy(gameObject); yield break; }
        op.allowSceneActivation = false;

        // 실제 진행(0~0.9)과 최소 표시 시간 중 느린 쪽을 따라 바가 부드럽게 찬다
        while (true)
        {
            float real = Mathf.Clamp01(op.progress / 0.9f);
            float timeGate = Mathf.Clamp01((Time.unscaledTime - start) / MinimumShow);
            float target = Mathf.Min(real, timeGate);
            shown = Mathf.MoveTowards(shown, target, Time.unscaledDeltaTime * 1.8f);
            SetProgress(shown);
            Animate(start);
            if (shown >= 0.999f && real >= 1f) break;
            yield return null;
        }

        op.allowSceneActivation = true;
        while (!op.isDone) { Animate(start); yield return null; }
        yield return null; // 새 장면의 Start가 한 번 돈 뒤에 걷어낸다

        for (float t = 0f; t < FadeOut; t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime)
        {
            _group.alpha = 1f - t / FadeOut;
            Animate(start);
            yield return null;
        }
        Destroy(gameObject);
    }

    // 점 1→2→3→1… 반복, 배경은 아주 천천히 확대
    private void Animate(float start)
    {
        float elapsed = Time.unscaledTime - start;
        int dots = 1 + (int)(elapsed / DotInterval) % 3;
        _label.text = "Loading" + new string('.', dots);
        if (_photo != null) _photo.localScale = Vector3.one * (1f + elapsed * 0.02f);
    }

    private void SetProgress(float value)
    {
        float inner = BarWidth - 12f;
        _fill.sizeDelta = new Vector2(Mathf.Max(0f, inner * value), _fill.sizeDelta.y);
        _fill.gameObject.SetActive(value > 0.01f);
    }

    private static Sprite PickBackground()
    {
        var candidates = new List<Sprite>();
        foreach (var sprite in Resources.LoadAll<Sprite>(OpeningPath))
        {
            if (sprite == null || Excluded.Contains(sprite.name)) continue;
            if (!int.TryParse(sprite.name, out _)) continue; // 번호가 아닌 그림(콘티 등)은 제외
            candidates.Add(sprite);
        }
        return candidates.Count > 0 ? candidates[Random.Range(0, candidates.Count)] : null;
    }

    private static RectTransform NewRect(string name, Transform parent)
    {
        var rect = (RectTransform)new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform)).transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        return rect;
    }

    private static Image NewImage(string name, Transform parent, Color color)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
        go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
        var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
        image.color = color;
        image.raycastTarget = false;
        return image;
    }

    private static void Stretch(RectTransform rect)
    {
        rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        rect.offsetMin = rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/ModalDialog.cs
using System;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
using UnityEngine.UI;

/// <summary>
/// ESC 메뉴(Resources/UI/PauseMenu)의 프레임과 버튼을 복제해 만드는 확인 팝업.
/// 디자인을 ESC 메뉴와 통일하고, 씬 파일을 수정하지 않고도 어느 씬에서나 띄울 수 있다.
/// 문구의 줄바꿈은 호출하는 쪽에서 "\n"으로 직접 넣는다. (한글은 글자 단위로 줄바꿈되어 한 글자만 남을 수 있음)
/// </summary>
public sealed class ModalDialog : MonoBehaviour
{
    public readonly struct Option
    {
        public readonly string Label;
        public readonly Action OnClick;
        public Option(string label, Action onClick) { Label = label; OnClick = onClick; }
    }

    // 버튼 1~2개: 안내문 그림(JSW/UI/Notice)에 그려져 있던 버튼 칸 2개의 자리. (본문 기준 y, 본문은 패널 기준 -40)
    private const float TwoButtonFirstY = -88f;
    private const float TwoButtonSpacing = 101f;
    // 버튼 3개 이상: 마지막 버튼이 그림의 하단 구분선(패널 기준 -306)과 겹치지 않도록 위로 올리고 간격을 좁힌다.
    private const float ManyButtonLastY = -210f;
    private const float ManyButtonSpacing = 92f;
    private const float ManyQuestionY = 125f;
    private const float ManyExplanationY = 62f;
    private const float ManyExplanationHeight = 70f;

    /// <summary>팝업이 열려 있으면 ESC 메뉴가 같은 키 입력으로 열리지 않게 한다.</summary>
    public static bool IsOpen => _current != null;
    /// <summary>ESC로 팝업을 닫은 프레임. 같은 프레임에 ESC 메뉴가 열리지 않게 한다.</summary>
    public static bool BlocksEscape => _current != null || Time.frameCount == _closedFrame;
    private static ModalDialog _current;
    private static int _closedFrame = -1;

    [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
    private static void ResetStatics() { _current = null; _closedFrame = -1; }

    private Action _onCancel;
    private bool _escapeHeld = true; // 팝업을 연 키 입력이 바로 닫기로 처리되지 않게 한다.

    /// <param name="onCancel">ESC로 닫을 때 실행. null이면 단순히 닫기만 한다.</param>
    public static ModalDialog Show(string heading, string question, string explanation, Action onCancel, params Option[] options)
    {
        if (_current != null) _current.Close();

        var prefab = Resources.Load<PauseMenuController>("UI/PauseMenu");
        if (prefab == null || prefab.overlay == null || prefab.restartConfirmation == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("확인 팝업에 쓸 Resources/UI/PauseMenu 프리팹을 찾지 못했습니다.");
            return null;
        }

        var root = new GameObject("ModalDialog", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Canvas), typeof(CanvasScaler), typeof(GraphicRaycaster));
        var canvas = root.GetComponent<Canvas>();
        canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
        canvas.sortingOrder = 600; // ESC 메뉴(500)보다 위
        var scaler = root.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
        scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
        scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1920, 1080);
        scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = 0.5f;

        var dialog = root.AddComponent<ModalDialog>();
        dialog._onCancel = onCancel;
        _current = dialog;

        var overlay = Instantiate(prefab.overlay, root.transform, false);
        overlay.SetActive(true);
        var panel = overlay.transform.Find("Frame/Panel");

        SetText(panel, "Heading", heading);
        // ESC 메뉴 전용 부제(MEMORIAL)는 확인 팝업에 어울리지 않아 숨긴다.
        var subtitle = panel.Find("Subtitle");
        if (subtitle != null) subtitle.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        var actions = panel.Find("Actions");
        if (actions != null) actions.gameObject.SetActive(false);

        var body = panel.Find("RestartConfirmation");
        body.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        SetText(body, "Question", question);
        SetText(body, "Explanation", explanation);

        var template = body.Find("ConfirmRestartButton");
        var cancelTemplate = body.Find("CancelRestartButton");
        if (cancelTemplate != null) cancelTemplate.gameObject.SetActive(false);

        float firstY = TwoButtonFirstY;
        float spacing = TwoButtonSpacing;
        if (options.Length > 2)
        {
            spacing = ManyButtonSpacing;
            firstY = ManyButtonLastY + spacing * (options.Length - 1);
            SetY(body, "Question", ManyQuestionY);
            if (SetY(body, "Explanation", ManyExplanationY) is RectTransform explanationRect)
                explanationRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(explanationRect.sizeDelta.x, ManyExplanationHeight);
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < options.Length; i++)
        {
            var option = options[i];
            var buttonObject = Instantiate(template.gameObject, body, false);
            buttonObject.name = "Option" + i;
            var rect = (RectTransform)buttonObject.transform;
            rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(rect.anchoredPosition.x, firstY - spacing * i);
            buttonObject.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true).text = option.Label;
            var button = buttonObject.GetComponent<Button>();
            button.onClick.RemoveAllListeners();
            button.onClick.AddListener(() =>
            {
                dialog.Close();
                option.OnClick?.Invoke();
            });
            UIHoverScale.Attach(buttonObject);
        }
        template.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        return dialog;
    }

    private static void SetText(Transform parent, string child, string value)
    {
        var target = parent.Find(child);
        if (target == null) return;
        var text = target.GetComponent<TMP_Text>();
        if (text == null) return;
        text.text = value ?? "";
        target.gameObject.SetActive(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value));
    }

    private static RectTransform SetY(Transform parent, string child, float y)
    {
        if (parent.Find(child) is not RectTransform rect) return null;
        rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(rect.anchoredPosition.x, y);
        return rect;
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        var keyboard = Keyboard.current;
        bool escape = keyboard != null && keyboard.escapeKey.isPressed;
        if (!escape) { _escapeHeld = false; return; }
        if (_escapeHeld) return;
        _escapeHeld = true;
        var cancel = _onCancel;
        Close();
        cancel?.Invoke();
    }

    public void Close()
    {
        _closedFrame = Time.frameCount;
        if (_current == this) _current = null;
        Destroy(gameObject);
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (_current == this) _current = null;
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/NpcGroupRoot.cs
using UnityEngine;

// 묶음 컨테이너 표시용.
public sealed class NpcGroupRoot : MonoBehaviour { }

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/NpcHoverEffect.cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

// 마을 화면 NPC 호버 연출.
// 1) 한 NPC가 여러 조각(예: 업적 NPC의 테이블 위 상반신 + 테이블 아래 다리)으로 나뉘어 있어도
//    어느 조각에 마우스를 올리든 NPC 전체가 하나의 중심점에서 함께 커진다.
// 2) 흰 실루엣 위에 같은 위치로 그려진 색 그림(JSW/Resources/캐릭터_색*.png)을 겹쳐
//    마우스를 올리면 서서히 색이 채워지고, 떼면 서서히 다시 하얘진다.
// 조각은 "같은 부모 + 같은 OnClick 함수"인 버튼끼리 한 NPC로 묶는다.
public sealed class NpcHoverEffect : MonoBehaviour
{
    // 흰 실루엣 스프라이트 시트 이름 → 같은 배치로 그려진 색 그림(Resources 경로). null이면 색 그림 없음(크기 효과만).
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> ColorSheets = new Dictionary<string, string>
    {
        { "button1_1", "캐릭터_색3" }, // 업적 NPC
        { "button1_2", null },          // 엔딩 후 업적 NPC (색 그림 없음)
        { "button2", "캐릭터_색2" },   // 캐릭터·아이템·스킨 상점 NPC
        { "button3", "캐릭터_색" },    // 추모·음악 NPC
        { "마을_튜토리얼NPC_흰", null },  // 튜토리얼 NPC (색 그림 없음, TutorialNpc.cs)
    };

    private const float HoverScale = 1.04f;
    private const float ColorFadeDuration = 0.5f;

    private readonly List<Group> groups = new List<Group>();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, Texture2D> colorTextures = new Dictionary<string, Texture2D>();

    public static void Setup(GameObject root)
    {
        if (root == null) return;
        var effect = root.GetComponent<NpcHoverEffect>();
        if (effect == null) effect = root.AddComponent<NpcHoverEffect>();
        effect.Build();
    }

    private void Build()
    {
        var byKey = new Dictionary<(Transform, string), List<Button>>();
        var order = new List<(Transform, string)>();
        foreach (var button in GetComponentsInChildren<Button>(true))
        {
            var image = button.targetGraphic as Image;
            if (image == null || image.sprite == null || image.sprite.texture == null) continue;
            if (!ColorSheets.ContainsKey(image.sprite.texture.name)) continue;
            if (button.GetComponentInParent<NpcGroupRoot>(true) != null) continue; // 이미 묶인 버튼

            var key = (button.transform.parent, ClickKey(button));
            if (!byKey.TryGetValue(key, out var list))
            {
                list = new List<Button>();
                byKey.Add(key, list);
                order.Add(key);
            }
            list.Add(button);
        }

        foreach (var key in order)
            groups.Add(CreateGroup(byKey[key]));
    }

    private static string ClickKey(Button button)
    {
        var onClick = button.onClick;
        if (onClick.GetPersistentEventCount() == 0) return button.name; // 함수가 없으면 단독 그룹
        return onClick.GetPersistentMethodName(0);
    }

    private Group CreateGroup(List<Button> buttons)
    {
        var parent = (RectTransform)buttons[0].transform.parent;

        // 부모와 같은 크기의 컨테이너에 조각들을 옮기고, 컨테이너의 pivot을 NPC 전체 중심에 둔다.
        // 앵커가 부모 기준 그대로 유지되므로 화면 크기가 바뀌어도 배치가 어긋나지 않는다.
        var containerObject = new GameObject(buttons[0].name + " (NPC)", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(NpcGroupRoot));
        containerObject.layer = parent.gameObject.layer;
        var container = (RectTransform)containerObject.transform;
        container.SetParent(parent, false);
        container.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        container.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        container.offsetMin = container.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;

        int siblingIndex = int.MaxValue;
        foreach (var button in buttons)
            siblingIndex = Mathf.Min(siblingIndex, button.transform.GetSiblingIndex());
        container.SetSiblingIndex(siblingIndex);

        var group = new Group { container = container };
        foreach (var button in buttons)
        {
            // 묶음이 크기를 담당하므로 개별 호버 확대는 끈다.
            var single = button.GetComponent<UIHoverScale>();
            if (single != null) Destroy(single);

            button.transform.SetParent(container, false);
            var piece = button.gameObject.AddComponent<NpcHoverPiece>();
            piece.group = group;
            group.buttons.Add(button);

            var overlay = CreateColorOverlay((Image)button.targetGraphic);
            if (overlay != null) group.overlays.Add(overlay);
        }

        UpdatePivot(group);
        return group;
    }

    private Image CreateColorOverlay(Image white)
    {
        var whiteSprite = white.sprite;
        var colorPath = ColorSheets[whiteSprite.texture.name];
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(colorPath)) return null;

        if (!colorTextures.TryGetValue(colorPath, out var colorTexture))
        {
            colorTexture = Resources.Load<Texture2D>(colorPath);
            colorTextures.Add(colorPath, colorTexture);
            if (colorTexture == null) Debug.LogWarning($"[NpcHoverEffect] 색 그림을 찾지 못했습니다: Resources/{colorPath}");
        }
        if (colorTexture == null) return null;

        // 흰 실루엣과 색 그림은 같은 크기·배치이므로 같은 영역을 잘라 쓴다.
        // (가져오기 설정 때문에 크기가 달라져도 비율로 맞춘다.)
        var whiteTexture = whiteSprite.texture;
        float sx = (float)colorTexture.width / whiteTexture.width;
        float sy = (float)colorTexture.height / whiteTexture.height;
        var r = whiteSprite.rect;
        var rect = new Rect(r.x * sx, r.y * sy, r.width * sx, r.height * sy);
        var pivot = new Vector2(whiteSprite.pivot.x / r.width, whiteSprite.pivot.y / r.height);
        var colorSprite = Sprite.Create(colorTexture, rect, pivot, whiteSprite.pixelsPerUnit * sx, 0, SpriteMeshType.FullRect);
        colorSprite.name = whiteSprite.name + " (color)";

        var overlayObject = new GameObject("Color", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
        overlayObject.layer = white.gameObject.layer;
        var overlayRect = (RectTransform)overlayObject.transform;
        overlayRect.SetParent(white.transform, false);
        overlayRect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        overlayRect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        overlayRect.offsetMin = overlayRect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;

        var overlay = overlayObject.GetComponent<Image>();
        overlay.sprite = colorSprite;
        overlay.type = white.type;
        overlay.preserveAspect = white.preserveAspect;
        overlay.raycastTarget = false;
        overlay.color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0f);
        return overlay;
    }

    private static void UpdatePivot(Group group)
    {
        // 조각들을 모두 감싸는 영역의 중심을 컨테이너 pivot으로 쓴다.
        var container = group.container;
        var min = new Vector2(float.MaxValue, float.MaxValue);
        var max = new Vector2(float.MinValue, float.MinValue);
        var corners = new Vector3[4];
        foreach (var button in group.buttons)
        {
            ((RectTransform)button.transform).GetWorldCorners(corners);
            foreach (var corner in corners)
            {
                Vector2 local = container.InverseTransformPoint(corner);
                min = Vector2.Min(min, local);
                max = Vector2.Max(max, local);
            }
        }
        var size = container.rect.size;
        if (size.x <= 0f || size.y <= 0f || min.x > max.x) return;

        var center = (min + max) * 0.5f;
        var newPivot = new Vector2((center.x - container.rect.xMin) / size.x, (center.y - container.rect.yMin) / size.y);
        // 앵커가 부모 전체로 늘어나 있고 오프셋이 0이라 pivot을 바꿔도 컨테이너 위치는 그대로다.
        container.pivot = newPivot;
        container.offsetMin = container.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        group.pivotSize = size;
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        float dt = Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
        foreach (var group in groups)
        {
            if (group.container == null) continue;

            if (!group.container.gameObject.activeInHierarchy)
            {
                // 화면이 바뀌어 꺼진 경우: 다음에 켜질 때 흰색·원래 크기에서 시작한다.
                group.hoverCount = 0;
                group.Apply(0f, 1f);
                continue;
            }

            if (group.container.rect.size != group.pivotSize) UpdatePivot(group);

            bool hovered = group.hoverCount > 0 && group.Interactable();
            group.colorT = Mathf.MoveTowards(group.colorT, hovered ? 1f : 0f, dt / ColorFadeDuration);

            float targetScale = hovered ? HoverScale : 1f;
            group.scale = Mathf.Lerp(group.scale, targetScale, 1f - Mathf.Exp(-20f * dt));
            if (Mathf.Abs(group.scale - targetScale) < 0.0001f) group.scale = targetScale;

            group.Apply(Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, group.colorT), group.scale);
        }
    }

    internal sealed class Group
    {
        public RectTransform container;
        public readonly List<Button> buttons = new List<Button>();
        public readonly List<Image> overlays = new List<Image>();
        public int hoverCount;
        public float colorT;
        public float scale = 1f;
        public Vector2 pivotSize;

        public bool Interactable()
        {
            foreach (var button in buttons)
                if (button != null && button.IsActive() && button.IsInteractable()) return true;
            return false;
        }

        public void Apply(float color, float s)
        {
            if (color <= 0f) colorT = 0f;
            if (s == 1f) scale = 1f;
            container.localScale = new Vector3(s, s, 1f);
            foreach (var overlay in overlays)
            {
                if (overlay == null) continue;
                var c = overlay.color;
                if (c.a == color) continue;
                c.a = color;
                overlay.color = c;
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/NpcHoverPiece.cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

// 각 조각의 마우스 진입/이탈을 묶음에 전달한다.
public sealed class NpcHoverPiece : MonoBehaviour, IPointerEnterHandler, IPointerExitHandler
{
    internal NpcHoverEffect.Group group;
    private bool inside;

    public void OnPointerEnter(PointerEventData eventData)
    {
        if (inside || group == null) return;
        inside = true;
        group.hoverCount++;
    }

    public void OnPointerExit(PointerEventData eventData) => Leave();
    private void OnDisable() => Leave();

    private void Leave()
    {
        if (!inside || group == null) return;
        inside = false;
        group.hoverCount = Mathf.Max(0, group.hoverCount - 1);
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/Plate.cs
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
using System.Net;
using UnityEngine.UIElements.Experimental;

public class Plate : MonoBehaviour
{
    private GameObject MovingPoint;
    private GameObject BossPlate;
    private List<GameObject> Player_spawnedList = new List<GameObject>();
    private List<GameObject> Boss_spawnedList = new List<GameObject>();
    float Player_x;
    float Player_y;
    SpriteRenderer sr;
    private Turn tn;
    private bool bossAttackDamageApplied;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (FindAnyObjectByType<BoardPanel>() != null)
        {
            enabled = false;
        }
    }

    void Start()
    {
        MovingPoint = GameObject.Find("MovePoint");
        BossPlate = GameObject.Find("BossPlate");
        sr = GetComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
        tn = FindAnyObjectByType<Turn>();
    }
    public void Check_Character(Player player)
    {
        if (player != null)
        { 
            Player_x = player.transform.position.x;
            Player_y = player.transform.position.y;
        }
    }

    public void Create_MovingPlate(int n)
    {
        int start = -(n / 2);

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
            {
                int dx = start + i;
                int dy = start + j;

                if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue; // 플레이어 자기 칸 제외

                float targetX = Player_x + dx;
                float targetY = Player_y + dy;

                int boardX = Mathf.RoundToInt(targetX - GameManager.BoardMinX);
                int boardY = Mathf.RoundToInt(targetY - GameManager.BoardMinY);

                bool isOccupied = false;

                // 보드 범위를 벗어나면 생성하지 않음
                if (targetX < GameManager.BoardMinX || targetX > GameManager.BoardMaxX ||
                    targetY < GameManager.BoardMinY || targetY > GameManager.BoardMaxY)
                {
                    continue;
                }
                for (int h = 0; h < 3; h++)
                {
                    if (Player.player_board_x[h] == boardX &&
                        Player.player_board_y[h] == boardY)
                    {
                        isOccupied = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (isOccupied)
                {
                    continue;
                } 



                GameObject obj = Instantiate(
                    MovingPoint,
                    new Vector2(targetX, targetY),
                    Quaternion.identity
                );
                Player_spawnedList.Add(obj);
            }
        }
    }

    public void Remove_MovingPlate()
    {
        foreach (GameObject obj in Player_spawnedList)
        {
            if (obj != null)
            {
                Destroy(obj, 0f);
            }
        }
        Player_spawnedList.Clear();
    }

    public void Boss_RemovePlate()
    {
        foreach (GameObject obj in Boss_spawnedList)
        {
            if (obj != null)
            {
                Destroy(obj, 0f);
            }
        }
        Boss_spawnedList.Clear();
        tn.Turn_On();
    }

 //대저택 보스

    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Plaza_C1() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.PlazaC1);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Plaza_C2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.PlazaC2);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Plaza_C3() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.PlazaC3);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Plaza_C4() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.PlazaC4);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Plaza_C5() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.PlazaC5);

    // 성당: A 2단계, B 2단계, C 3단계. 밝은 칸에 공격 Plate를 생성한다.
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Cathedral_A1() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.CathedralA1);

    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Door_A2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.DoorA2);

    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Door_B2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.DoorB2);

    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Library_A2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.LibraryA2);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Library_B1() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.LibraryB1);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Library_B2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.LibraryB2);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Library_C1() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.LibraryC1);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Library_C2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.LibraryC2);

    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Training_B1() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.TrainingB1);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Training_B2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.TrainingB2);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Training_C1() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.TrainingC1);
    public void Boss_CreatePlate_Training_C2() => Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern.TrainingC2);

    private void Boss_CreatePlate(BattlePatternRules.Pattern pattern)
    {
        bossAttackDamageApplied = false;
        foreach (var cell in BattlePatternRules.GetDangerCells(pattern))
        {
            // UI는 위에서 y=0, 월드 보드는 아래에서 y=0이다.
            int worldY = BattlePatternRules.BoardSize - 1 - cell.y;
            var obj = Instantiate(BossPlate, GameManager.position[cell.x, worldY], Quaternion.identity);
            Boss_spawnedList.Add(obj);
            Player_hit(cell.x, worldY);
        }
        Invoke(nameof(Boss_RemovePlate), 1f);
    }

    //대저택 보스
    public void Player_hit(int x, int y)
    {
        if (bossAttackDamageApplied) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        {
            if (x == Player.player_board_x[i])
            {
                if (y == Player.player_board_y[i])
                {
                    GameManager.PlayerHP -= 5;
                    bossAttackDamageApplied = true;
                    Debug.Log("현제 플레이어 HP : " + GameManager.PlayerHP);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
}

/// <summary>
/// JSW 전투 규칙의 단일 원본입니다. 월드 오브젝트와 UI 보드 모두 이 좌표 규칙을 공유합니다.
/// </summary>
public static class BattlePatternRules
{
    public const int BoardSize = 9;

    public enum ColorEffect
    {
        None,
        Relocate,
        ReduceNextActionPoints,
        DamageBossOnDodge,
        IncreaseKingWillPower,
        HealKing,
        RelocateKingTargets
    }

    public static ColorEffect GetColorEffect(Pattern pattern) => pattern switch
    {
        Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A3 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A4 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2C2 => ColorEffect.Relocate,
        Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B2 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B1 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B3 => ColorEffect.ReduceNextActionPoints,
        Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B3 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C3 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A3 => ColorEffect.DamageBossOnDodge,
        Pattern.KingB3 => ColorEffect.IncreaseKingWillPower,
        Pattern.KingA2 => ColorEffect.HealKing,
        Pattern.KingC4 => ColorEffect.RelocateKingTargets,
        _ => ColorEffect.None
    };

    // 배치되지 않은 칸과 피격 유닛의 이전 칸을 포함해, 예약된 칸을 제외한 위치를 추첨한다.
    public static List<Vector2Int> PickRelocationCells(int count, HashSet<Vector2Int> occupied)
    {
        var available = new List<Vector2Int>();
        for (int y = 0; y < BoardSize; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < BoardSize; x++)
            {
                var cell = new Vector2Int(x, y);
                if (!occupied.Contains(cell)) available.Add(cell);
            }
        var result = new List<Vector2Int>();
        while (result.Count < count && available.Count > 0)
        {
            int index = Random.Range(0, available.Count);
            result.Add(available[index]);
            available.RemoveAt(index);
        }
        return result;
    }

    public enum Pattern
    {
        Corners,
        Cross,
        SideColumns,
        MiddleRows,
        CenterSquare,
        Border,
        PlazaA1, PlazaA2,
        PlazaB1, PlazaB2, PlazaB3,
        PlazaC1, PlazaC2, PlazaC3, PlazaC4, PlazaC5,
        MansionA1, MansionA2,
        None,
        CathedralA1, CathedralA2,
        CathedralB1, CathedralB2,
        CathedralC1, CathedralC2, CathedralC3,
        DoorA1, DoorA2, DoorB1, DoorB2, DoorB3, DoorB4, DoorB5, DoorC1, DoorC2, DoorC3, DoorC4,
        BasementA1, BasementA2, BasementA3, BasementA4, BasementB1, BasementB2, BasementB3, BasementB4, BasementB5, BasementC1, BasementC2, BasementC3,
        LibraryA1, LibraryA2, LibraryB1, LibraryB2, LibraryC1, LibraryC2,
        TrainingA1, TrainingA2, TrainingA3, TrainingB1, TrainingB2, TrainingC1, TrainingC2,
        // 기존 enum 값은 에셋에 직렬화되므로 새 패턴은 끝에 추가한다.
        AfterlifePhase1A1, AfterlifePhase1A2, AfterlifePhase1A3,
        AfterlifePhase1B1, AfterlifePhase1B2, AfterlifePhase1B3,
        AfterlifePhase1C1, AfterlifePhase1C2, AfterlifePhase1C3,
        AfterlifePhase2A1, AfterlifePhase2A2, AfterlifePhase2A3, AfterlifePhase2A4,
        AfterlifePhase2B1, AfterlifePhase2B2, AfterlifePhase2B3, AfterlifePhase2B4,
        AfterlifePhase2C1, AfterlifePhase2C2,
        KingA1, KingA2, KingA3,
        KingB1, KingB2, KingB3,
        KingC1, KingC2, KingC3, KingC4,
        BellkeeperA1, BellkeeperA2, BellkeeperA3,
        BellkeeperB1, BellkeeperB2, BellkeeperB3,
        BellkeeperC1, BellkeeperC2, BellkeeperC3
    }

    public static HashSet<Vector2Int> GetDangerCells(Pattern pattern)
    {
        var cells = new HashSet<Vector2Int>();

        for (int x = 0; x < BoardSize; x++)
        {
            for (int y = 0; y < BoardSize; y++)
            {
                var isDangerous = pattern switch
                {
                    Pattern.Corners => (x / 3 != 1) && (y / 3 != 1),
                    Pattern.Cross => x / 3 == 1 || y / 3 == 1,
                    Pattern.SideColumns => x < 3 || x > 5,
                    Pattern.MiddleRows => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.CenterSquare => x is >= 2 and <= 6 && y is >= 2 and <= 6,
                    Pattern.Border => x < 2 || x > 6 || y < 2 || y > 6,
                    Pattern.PlazaA1 => y < 3,
                    Pattern.PlazaA2 => x is >= 2 and <= 6 && y is >= 2 and <= 6,
                    Pattern.PlazaB1 => x % 3 == 1,
                    Pattern.PlazaB2 => y % 3 == 1,
                    Pattern.PlazaB3 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.PlazaC1 => x == 0 || x == 8,
                    Pattern.PlazaC2 => x < 2 || x > 6,
                    Pattern.PlazaC3 => x < 3 || x > 5,
                    Pattern.PlazaC4 => x != 4,
                    Pattern.PlazaC5 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.MansionA1 => (x / 3 != 1 && y / 3 != 1) || (x / 3 == 1 && y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.MansionA2 => (x / 3 == 1) != (y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.CathedralA1 => x == y || x + y == 8,
                    Pattern.CathedralA2 => x == 4 || y == 4,
                    // 중앙 3x3 테두리 8칸만 안전하며, 정중앙 한 칸은 공격한다.
                    Pattern.CathedralB1 => x < 3 || x > 5 || y < 3 || y > 5 || (x == 4 && y == 4),
                    Pattern.CathedralB2 => x == 0 || x == 8 || y == 0 || y == 8,
                    Pattern.CathedralC1 => y is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.CathedralC2 => y < 3,
                    Pattern.CathedralC3 => y > 5,
                    Pattern.DoorA1 => x < 3 || x > 5 || y < 3 || y > 5 || (x == 4 && y == 4),
                    Pattern.DoorA2 => x < 2 || x > 6 || y < 2 || y > 6,
                    Pattern.DoorB1 => x == 0 || x == 8,
                    Pattern.DoorB2 => x < 2 || x > 6,
                    Pattern.DoorB3 => x < 3 || x > 5,
                    Pattern.DoorB4 => x != 4,
                    Pattern.DoorB5 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.DoorC1 => (x / 3 != 1 && y / 3 != 1) || (x / 3 == 1 && y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.DoorC2 => (x / 3 == 1) != (y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.DoorC3 => x < 3 || x > 5,
                    Pattern.DoorC4 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.BasementA1 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.BasementA2 => x < 3,
                    Pattern.BasementA3 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.BasementA4 => x > 5,
                    Pattern.BasementB1 => x == 0 || x == 8,
                    Pattern.BasementB2 => x < 2 || x > 6,
                    Pattern.BasementB3 => x < 3 || x > 5,
                    Pattern.BasementB4 => x != 4,
                    Pattern.BasementB5 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.BasementC1 => x % 4 != 0,
                    Pattern.BasementC2 => y % 4 != 0,
                    Pattern.BasementC3 => x is >= 2 and <= 6 && y is >= 2 and <= 6,
                    Pattern.LibraryA1 => x % 4 != 0,
                    Pattern.LibraryA2 => y % 4 != 0,
                    Pattern.LibraryB1 => x == y || x + y == 8,
                    Pattern.LibraryB2 => x == 4 || y == 4,
                    Pattern.LibraryC1 => x == 0 || x == 8 || y == 0 || y == 8,
                    Pattern.LibraryC2 => x is >= 1 and <= 7 && y is >= 1 and <= 7,
                    Pattern.TrainingA1 => x == y || x + y == 8,
                    Pattern.TrainingA2 => x == 4 || y == 4,
                    Pattern.TrainingA3 => x != 4,
                    Pattern.TrainingB1 => (x / 3 != 1 && y / 3 != 1) || (x / 3 == 1 && y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.TrainingB2 => (x / 3 == 1) != (y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.TrainingC1 => x is >= 2 and <= 6 && y is >= 2 and <= 6,
                    Pattern.TrainingC2 => x < 2 || x > 6 || y < 2 || y > 6,
                    // 저승: 색상별 기믹 없이 밝은 영역을 공격 칸으로 해석한다.
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A1 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B2 =>
                        (x / 3 != 1 && y / 3 != 1) || (x / 3 == 1 && y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A2 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B4 =>
                        (x / 3 == 1) != (y / 3 == 1),
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A3 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A4 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2C2 => true,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B1 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2C1 => y < 3,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B2 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B1 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B3 => x < 3 || x > 5,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B3 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C3 or Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A3 =>
                        x >= 1 && x <= 7 && y >= 1 && y <= 7,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C1 => x == y || x + y == 8,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C2 => x == 4 || y == 4,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A1 => x < 3,
                    Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A2 => x > 5,
                    // 알현실 참고도: 밝은 칸이 공격 범위. A2는 바깥 1칸과 중앙 5x5.
                    Pattern.KingA1 => x is >= 3 and <= 5,
                    Pattern.KingA2 => x == 0 || x == 8 || y == 0 || y == 8
                        || (x is >= 2 and <= 6 && y is >= 2 and <= 6),
                    Pattern.KingA3 => x < 2 || x > 6,
                    Pattern.KingB1 => x == y || x + y == 8,
                    Pattern.KingB2 => x == 4 || y == 4,
                    Pattern.KingB3 => x < 3 || x > 5 || y < 3 || y > 5,
                    Pattern.KingC1 => x % 4 != 0,
                    Pattern.KingC2 => y % 4 != 0,
                    Pattern.KingC3 => x is >= 2 and <= 6 && y is >= 2 and <= 6,
                    Pattern.KingC4 => true,
                    // Three tolls: inward echoes, swinging chain, then a funeral cross.
                    Pattern.BellkeeperA1 => x < 2 || x > 6 || y < 2 || y > 6,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperA2 => x >= 2 && x <= 6 && y >= 2 && y <= 6
                        && (x == 2 || x == 6 || y == 2 || y == 6),
                    Pattern.BellkeeperA3 => x >= 3 && x <= 5 && y >= 3 && y <= 5,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperB1 => x < 3,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperB2 => x > 5,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperB3 => x >= 3 && x <= 5,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperC1 => System.Math.Abs(x - y) <= 1,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperC2 => System.Math.Abs(x + y - 8) <= 1,
                    Pattern.BellkeeperC3 => x == 4 || y == 4,
                    _ => false
                };

                if (isDangerous)
                {
                    cells.Add(new Vector2Int(x, y));
                }
            }
        }

        return cells;
    }

    public static Pattern GetDefaultPattern(int turnCount)
    {
        return (Pattern)((turnCount - 1) % 6);
    }

    // 전투마다 별도 인스턴스를 사용한다. 한 묶음이 끝난 뒤에만 다시 추첨한다.
    public sealed class MansionSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public MansionSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.MansionA1, Pattern.MansionA2 },
            new[] { Pattern.SideColumns, Pattern.MiddleRows },
            new[] { Pattern.CenterSquare, Pattern.Border }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class PlazaSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public PlazaSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.PlazaA1, Pattern.PlazaA2 },
            new[] { Pattern.PlazaB1, Pattern.PlazaB2, Pattern.PlazaB3 },
            new[] { Pattern.PlazaC1, Pattern.PlazaC2, Pattern.PlazaC3, Pattern.PlazaC4, Pattern.PlazaC5 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class CathedralSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public CathedralSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.CathedralA1, Pattern.CathedralA2 },
            new[] { Pattern.CathedralB1, Pattern.CathedralB2 },
            new[] { Pattern.CathedralC1, Pattern.CathedralC2, Pattern.CathedralC3 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class DoorSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public DoorSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.DoorA1, Pattern.DoorA2 },
            new[] { Pattern.DoorB1, Pattern.DoorB2, Pattern.DoorB3, Pattern.DoorB4, Pattern.DoorB5 },
            new[] { Pattern.DoorC1, Pattern.DoorC2, Pattern.DoorC3, Pattern.DoorC4 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class BasementSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public BasementSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.BasementA1, Pattern.BasementA2, Pattern.BasementA3, Pattern.BasementA4 },
            new[] { Pattern.BasementB1, Pattern.BasementB2, Pattern.BasementB3, Pattern.BasementB4, Pattern.BasementB5 },
            new[] { Pattern.BasementC1, Pattern.BasementC2, Pattern.BasementC3 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class LibrarySequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public LibrarySequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.LibraryA1, Pattern.LibraryA2 },
            new[] { Pattern.LibraryB1, Pattern.LibraryB2 },
            new[] { Pattern.LibraryC1, Pattern.LibraryC2 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class TrainingSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public TrainingSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.TrainingA1, Pattern.TrainingA2, Pattern.TrainingA3 },
            new[] { Pattern.TrainingB1, Pattern.TrainingB2 },
            new[] { Pattern.TrainingC1, Pattern.TrainingC2 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class AfterlifePhase1Sequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public AfterlifePhase1Sequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A1, Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A2, Pattern.AfterlifePhase1A3 },
            new[] { Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B1, Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B2, Pattern.AfterlifePhase1B3 },
            new[] { Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C1, Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C2, Pattern.AfterlifePhase1C3 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class AfterlifePhase2Sequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public AfterlifePhase2Sequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A1, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A2, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A3, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2A4 },
            new[] { Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B1, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B2, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B3, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2B4 },
            new[] { Pattern.AfterlifePhase2C1, Pattern.AfterlifePhase2C2 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class KingSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        public KingSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.KingA1, Pattern.KingA2, Pattern.KingA3 },
            new[] { Pattern.KingB1, Pattern.KingB2, Pattern.KingB3 },
            new[] { Pattern.KingC1, Pattern.KingC2, Pattern.KingC3, Pattern.KingC4 }
        }) { }
    }

    public sealed class BellkeeperSequence : PatternSequence
    {
        // Every group has three steps, keeping the opening aligned with turn % 3.
        public BellkeeperSequence() : base(new[]
        {
            new[] { Pattern.BellkeeperA1, Pattern.BellkeeperA2, Pattern.BellkeeperA3 },
            new[] { Pattern.BellkeeperB1, Pattern.BellkeeperB2, Pattern.BellkeeperB3 },
            new[] { Pattern.BellkeeperC1, Pattern.BellkeeperC2, Pattern.BellkeeperC3 }
        }) { }
    }

    public abstract class PatternSequence
    {
        private readonly Pattern[][] Groups;

        protected PatternSequence(Pattern[][] groups)
        {
            Groups = groups;
        }

        private int group = -1;
        private int step;

        // Next() selects a step; this becomes true before that final step is executed.
        public bool IsLastStep => group >= 0 && step == Groups[group].Length;

        public void Reset()
        {
            group = -1;
            step = 0;
        }

        public Pattern Next()
        {
            if (group < 0 || step >= Groups[group].Length)
            {
                group = Random.Range(0, Groups.Length);
                step = 0;
            }
            return Groups[group][step++];
        }
    }
}
