        if (_hud != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(_hud.gameObject);
        if (_report != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(_report);
    }
}

/// <summary>시험 규칙 전용 화면: 종소리 신호, 패배 분석.</summary>
public sealed class TrialHud : MonoBehaviour
{
    private BoardPanel _board;
    private TextMeshProUGUI _title;
    private TextMeshProUGUI _hint;

    public static TrialHud Create(BoardPanel board)
    {
        var root = NewCanvas("BellkeeperTrialHud", 40);
        var hud = root.AddComponent<TrialHud>();
        hud._board = board;

        // 오른쪽 보스 영역, HP 막대 아래에 둔다(가운데 판과 로그를 가리지 않게).
        var panel = NewBox(root.transform, "Signal", new Vector2(1f, 1f), new Vector2(-290f, -280f), new Vector2(540f, 140f), new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.78f));
        hud._title = NewText(panel.transform, "Title", board.UiFont, 30f, Color.white, new Vector2(0f, 38f), new Vector2(520f, 44f));
        hud._hint = NewText(panel.transform, "Hint", board.UiFont, 20f, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.85f), new Vector2(0f, -20f), new Vector2(510f, 76f));
        hud._hint.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;
        return hud;
    }

    public void SetSignal(string title, string hint, int phase, int willPower)
    {
        if (_title != null) _title.text = $"{title}  <size=70%>/ {phase}페이즈</size>";
        if (_hint != null) _hint.text = $"{hint}\n<color=#FFB080>이번 턴 끝 의지력 피해 {willPower}</color>";
    }

    private bool _shown = true;

    private void Update()
    {
        if (_board == null) return;
        // 승리/패배 화면을 가리지 않도록 전투가 끝나면 숨긴다.
        bool show = !_board.IsBattleOver;
        if (show != _shown)
        {
            _shown = show;
            foreach (Transform child in transform) child.gameObject.SetActive(show);
        }
    }

    public static GameObject CreateReport(BoardPanel board, string body)
    {
        var root = NewCanvas("BellkeeperTrialReport", 45);
        var dim = NewBox(root.transform, "Dim", new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), Vector2.zero, new Vector2(4000f, 4000f), new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.55f));
        dim.GetComponent<Image>().raycastTarget = true;
        var box = NewBox(root.transform, "Box", new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), Vector2.zero, new Vector2(900f, 760f), new Color(0.05f, 0.05f, 0.05f, 1f));
        var text = NewText(box.transform, "Body", board.UiFont, 24f, Color.white, new Vector2(0f, 30f), new Vector2(820f, 640f));
        text.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft;
        text.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;
        text.text = body;
        var close = NewBox(box.transform, "Close", new Vector2(0.5f, 0f), new Vector2(0f, 50f), new Vector2(260f, 60f), new Color(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1f));
        NewText(close.transform, "Label", board.UiFont, 24f, Color.black, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(250f, 56f)).text = "확인";
        close.GetComponent<Image>().raycastTarget = true;
        close.AddComponent<Button>().onClick.AddListener(() => Destroy(root));
        return root;
    }

    private static GameObject NewCanvas(string name, int order)
    {
        // 화면 기준으로 배치하려면 다른 Canvas 안에 넣지 않는다(씬을 떠나면 함께 사라진다).
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Canvas), typeof(CanvasScaler), typeof(GraphicRaycaster));
        var canvas = go.GetComponent<Canvas>();
        canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
        canvas.overrideSorting = true;
        canvas.sortingOrder = order;
        var scaler = go.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
        scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
        scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1920, 1080);
        scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = 0.5f;
        return go;
    }

    private static GameObject NewBox(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 anchor, Vector2 pos, Vector2 size, Color color)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = anchor;
        rect.anchoredPosition = pos;
        rect.sizeDelta = size;
        var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
        image.color = color;
        image.raycastTarget = false;
        return go;
    }

    private static TextMeshProUGUI NewText(Transform parent, string name, TMP_FontAsset font, float size, Color color, Vector2 pos, Vector2 box)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(TextMeshProUGUI));
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        rect.anchoredPosition = pos;
        rect.sizeDelta = box;
        var text = go.GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
        if (font != null) text.font = font;
        text.fontSize = size;
        text.color = color;
        text.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
        text.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
        text.raycastTarget = false;
        return text;
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/Trial/UnitInfoView.cs
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

/// <summary>
/// 전투 화면 왼쪽 아래 캐릭터 초상화의 정보 표시.
/// - 마우스를 올리면 초상화가 어두워지며 &lt;정보&gt; 글자가 뜨고, 초상화 위에 정보창(대사창 테두리)이 뜬다.
/// - 정보창 오른쪽 위에 피격 해골 3개 (맞은 만큼 붉게, 3번째에 쓰러짐).
/// </summary>
public sealed class UnitInfoView
{
    private const float PanelWidth = 520f;
    private const float Padding = 26f;
    private const float TitleHeight = 42f;
    private const float BodyLineSpacing = -6f;
    private const float PipSize = 30f;
    private const float PipGap = 6f;

    private readonly Image[] _portraits;
    private readonly RectTransform _canvas;
    private readonly TMP_FontAsset _font;
    private readonly GameObject[] _dims;
    private readonly Sprite _skullHit, _skullEmpty;

    private RectTransform _panel;
    private TextMeshProUGUI _title, _body;
    private Image[] _panelPips;

    public int Shown { get; private set; } = -1;

    public UnitInfoView(Image[] portraits, TMP_FontAsset font)
    {
        _portraits = portraits;
        _font = font;
        _canvas = (RectTransform)portraits[0].canvas.rootCanvas.transform;
        _skullHit = Resources.Load<Sprite>("UI/skull_hit");
        _skullEmpty = Resources.Load<Sprite>("UI/skull_empty");
        _dims = new GameObject[portraits.Length];
        for (int i = 0; i < portraits.Length; i++)
        {
            _dims[i] = CreateDim(portraits[i].transform);
        }
        CreatePanel();
    }

    // ───────── 초상화 어둡게 + <정보> ─────────

    private GameObject CreateDim(Transform portrait)
    {
        var go = new GameObject("InfoDim", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(portrait, false);
        rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero; rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        rect.offsetMin = rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
        image.color = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.55f);
        image.raycastTarget = false;

        var label = NewText(rect, "Label", 34f, Color.white);
        label.richText = false;
        label.text = "<정보>";
        label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
        Stretch(label.rectTransform);
        go.SetActive(false);
        return go;
    }

    // ───────── 해골 줄 ─────────

    private Image[] CreatePipRow(Transform parent, Vector2 anchor, Vector2 pivot, Vector2 offset, float size)
    {
        var row = new GameObject("HitPips", typeof(RectTransform));
        var rect = (RectTransform)row.transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = anchor;
        rect.pivot = pivot;
        rect.anchoredPosition = offset;
        rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(size * 3f + PipGap * 2f, size);
        var pips = new Image[3];
        for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
        {
            var go = new GameObject("Skull" + k, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
            var r = (RectTransform)go.transform;
            r.SetParent(rect, false);
            r.anchorMin = r.anchorMax = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
            r.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
            r.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(k * (size + PipGap), 0f);
            r.sizeDelta = new Vector2(size, size);
            pips[k] = go.GetComponent<Image>();
            pips[k].preserveAspect = true;
            pips[k].raycastTarget = false;
            pips[k].sprite = _skullEmpty;
        }
        row.SetActive(false);
        return pips;
    }

    /// <summary>hits: 맞은 횟수. hits &lt; 0 이면 해골 줄을 숨긴다.</summary>
    public void SetPips(int i, int hits)
    {
        if (i == Shown) Apply(_panelPips, hits);
    }

    private void Apply(Image[] pips, int hits)
    {
        bool show = hits >= 0 && _skullHit != null;
        var row = pips[0].transform.parent.gameObject;
        if (row.activeSelf != show) row.SetActive(show);
        if (!show) return;
        for (int k = 0; k < pips.Length; k++)
        {
            var sprite = k < hits ? _skullHit : _skullEmpty;
            if (pips[k].sprite != sprite) pips[k].sprite = sprite;
        }
    }

    // ───────── 정보창 ─────────

    private void CreatePanel()
    {
        var go = new GameObject("UnitInfoPanel", typeof(RectTransform));
        _panel = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        _panel.SetParent(_canvas, false);
        _panel.anchorMin = _panel.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
        _panel.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0f);
        // 전투 화면의 다른 캔버스보다 위에 그린다.
        var overlay = go.AddComponent<Canvas>();
        overlay.overrideSorting = true;
        overlay.sortingOrder = 500;

        var fill = new GameObject("Fill", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
        var fr = (RectTransform)fill.transform;
        fr.SetParent(_panel, false);
        Stretch(fr, 8f);
        var fillImage = fill.GetComponent<Image>();
        fillImage.color = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.92f);
        fillImage.raycastTarget = false;

        var sprites = Resources.LoadAll<Sprite>("대사창");
        if (sprites.Length > 0)
        {
            var frame = new GameObject("Frame", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
            var r = (RectTransform)frame.transform;
            r.SetParent(_panel, false);
            Stretch(r);
            var image = frame.GetComponent<Image>();
            image.sprite = sprites[0];
            image.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
            image.pixelsPerUnitMultiplier = 1.3f;
            image.raycastTarget = false;
        }

        _title = NewText(_panel, "Title", 34f, Color.white);
        var tr = _title.rectTransform;
        tr.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 1f); tr.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
        tr.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
        tr.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(Padding, -Padding + 6f);
        tr.sizeDelta = new Vector2(-Padding * 2f, TitleHeight);

        _panelPips = CreatePipRow(_panel, new Vector2(1f, 1f), new Vector2(1f, 1f), new Vector2(-Padding, -Padding + 2f), PipSize);

        _body = NewText(_panel, "Body", 25f, new Color(0.9f, 0.9f, 0.92f));
        _body.richText = true;
        _body.lineSpacing = BodyLineSpacing;
        var br = _body.rectTransform;
        br.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 1f); br.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
        br.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
        br.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(Padding, -Padding - TitleHeight + 6f);
        br.sizeDelta = new Vector2(-Padding * 2f, 100f);

        go.SetActive(false);
    }

    public void Show(int i)
    {
        if (Shown >= 0 && Shown != i) Hide(Shown);
        Shown = i;
        _dims[i].SetActive(true);
        _panel.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        _panel.SetAsLastSibling();
    }

    public void Hide(int i)
    {
        if (i >= 0 && i < _dims.Length) _dims[i].SetActive(false);
        if (Shown != i) return;
        Shown = -1;
        _panel.gameObject.SetActive(false);
    }

    public void SetContent(string title, string body, int hits)
    {
        if (Shown < 0) return;
        _title.text = title;
        _body.text = body;
        Apply(_panelPips, hits);

        float innerWidth = PanelWidth - Padding * 2f;
        float bodyHeight = _body.GetPreferredValues(body, innerWidth, 0f).y;
        _body.rectTransform.sizeDelta = new Vector2(-Padding * 2f, bodyHeight);
        float height = Padding * 2f + TitleHeight - 6f + bodyHeight;
        _panel.sizeDelta = new Vector2(PanelWidth, height);

        // 마우스를 올린 초상화의 가운데 위에 띄우고, 화면 밖으로 나가지 않게 한다.
        var portrait = _portraits[Shown].rectTransform;
        var corners = new Vector3[4];
        portrait.GetWorldCorners(corners);
        Vector3 topCenter = (corners[1] + corners[2]) * 0.5f;
        Vector2 local = _canvas.InverseTransformPoint(topCenter);
        var half = _canvas.rect.size * 0.5f;
        float x = Mathf.Clamp(local.x, -half.x + PanelWidth * 0.5f + 8f, half.x - PanelWidth * 0.5f - 8f);
        float y = Mathf.Min(local.y + 12f, half.y - height - 8f);
        _panel.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(x, y);
    }

    // ───────── 공용 ─────────

    private TextMeshProUGUI NewText(Transform parent, string name, float size, Color color)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(TextMeshProUGUI));
        go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
        var text = go.GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
        if (_font != null) text.font = _font;
        text.fontSize = size;
        text.color = color;
        text.raycastTarget = false;
        text.enableWordWrapping = true;
        text.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft;
        return text;
    }

    private static void Stretch(RectTransform rect, float inset = 0f)
    {
        rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero; rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        rect.offsetMin = new Vector2(inset, inset);
        rect.offsetMax = new Vector2(-inset, -inset);
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/Tutorial/TutorialDirector.cs
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;

/// <summary>
/// 튜토리얼 진행자. TutorialScript의 단계를 차례로 보여 주고, 말풍선이 시킨 행동만 허락한다.
/// - 입력 막기: BattleManagerTemp가 배치·드래그·이동·심판 전에 Allow를 묻는다.
/// - 진행: 행동이 끝나면 Notify, 턴의 배치/행동 단계가 시작되면 OnPhase가 불린다.
/// - 말풍선 배경: Resources/대사창 (흰 테두리, 안쪽은 비어 있어 어두운 판을 깐다).
/// </summary>
public sealed class TutorialDirector : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static TutorialDirector Instance { get; private set; }
    public static bool Active => Instance != null;

    private const float BubbleWidth = 760f;
    private const float Gap = 24f;

    private BoardPanel _board;
    private BattleManagerTemp _battle;
    private int _index;
    private int _turn;
    private bool _place;
    private bool _showing;
    private bool _finishing;
    private int _endIndex;

    private const float FocusPad = 0f;   // 칸에 딱 맞게
    private const float DimAlpha = 0.72f;

    private RectTransform _root, _bubble, _arrow;
    private readonly RectTransform[] _dim = new RectTransform[4];
    private TextMeshProUGUI _text, _hint;
    private CanvasGroup _bubbleGroup;

    private TutorialStep Current => _index < TutorialScript.Steps.Length ? TutorialScript.Steps[_index] : null;

    public static void Create()
    {
        if (Instance != null) Destroy(Instance.gameObject);
        new GameObject("TutorialDirector").AddComponent<TutorialDirector>();
    }

    private void Awake()
    {
        Instance = this;
        BuildUi();
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (Instance == this) Instance = null;
    }

    // ───────────────────────── 입력 막기 · 진행 ─────────────────────────

    /// <summary>지금 이 행동을 해도 되는가. 튜토리얼이 아니면 항상 허락한다.</summary>
    public static bool Allow(TutorialAct act, int unit, Vector2Int? cell = null)
    {
        if (!Active) return true;
        var s = Instance._showing ? Instance.Current : null;
        if (s == null) return false;
        switch (act)
        {
            case TutorialAct.Select: return (s.act == TutorialAct.Select || s.act == TutorialAct.Place) && s.unit == unit;
            case TutorialAct.Place: return s.act == TutorialAct.Place && s.unit == unit && cell == s.cell;
            case TutorialAct.Drag: return s.act == TutorialAct.Move && s.unit == unit;
            case TutorialAct.Move: return s.act == TutorialAct.Move && s.unit == unit && cell == s.cell;
            case TutorialAct.Judge: return s.act == TutorialAct.Judge && s.unit == unit;
            default: return false;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>행동이 실제로 일어난 뒤 부른다. 시킨 행동이면 다음 단계로.</summary>
    public static void Notify(TutorialAct act, int unit)
    {
        if (!Active) return;
        var s = Instance.Current;
        if (!Instance._showing || s == null || s.act != act || s.unit != unit) return;
        Instance.Advance();
    }

    /// <summary>BoardPanel.NextTurn: 배치 단계(place) 또는 행동 단계가 시작됐다.</summary>
    public static void OnPhase(int turn, bool place)
    {
        if (!Active) return;
        Instance._turn = turn;
        Instance._place = place;
        Instance.TryShow();
    }

    /// <summary>같은 턴에 이어질 대사가 남았으면 보스 공격(턴 끝)을 미룬다.</summary>
    public static bool HoldsTurnEnd
    {
        get
        {
            if (!Active || Instance._finishing || !Instance._showing) return false;
            var s = Instance.Current;
            return s != null && s.act == TutorialAct.Next && !s.place && s.turn == Instance._turn && !Instance._place;
        }
    }

    private void Advance()
    {
        _index++;
        _showing = false;
        TryShow();
        // 미뤄 둔 턴 끝이 있으면 이어서 진행
        if (!_place && !HoldsTurnEnd && _board != null && _board.actionPoint <= 0 && _board.turn == BoardPanel.ETurn.Player) _board.TutorialContinue();
    }

    private void TryShow()
    {
        if (_finishing) return;
        // 지난 턴(또는 지난 배치 단계)에 남은 단계는 건너뛴다
        while (Current != null && (Current.turn < _turn || (Current.turn == _turn && Current.place && !_place))) _index++;
        var s = Current;
        if (s == null || s.turn != _turn || s.place != _place) { Hide(); return; }
        _showing = true;
        _text.text = s.text;
        _hint.gameObject.SetActive(s.act == TutorialAct.Next);
        // 행동 단계에서는 말풍선이 클릭·드래그를 가로채지 않게 한다.
        _bubbleGroup.blocksRaycasts = s.act == TutorialAct.Next;
        foreach (var d in _dim) d.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        _bubble.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        LayoutRebuilder.ForceRebuildLayoutImmediate(_bubble);
        Place();
    }

    private void Hide()
    {
        _showing = false;
        _bubble.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        _arrow.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        foreach (var d in _dim) d.gameObject.SetActive(false);
    }

    private void OnBubbleClicked()
    {
        if (_finishing)
        {
            _endIndex++;
            if (_endIndex >= TutorialScript.EndLines.Length) { Leave(); return; }
            _text.text = TutorialScript.EndLines[_endIndex];
            LayoutRebuilder.ForceRebuildLayoutImmediate(_bubble);
            return;
        }
        var s = Current;
        if (_showing && s != null && s.act == TutorialAct.Next) Advance();
    }

    /// <summary>안내자를 쓰러뜨렸다. 마지막 말을 보여 주고 클릭하면 마을로.</summary>
    public void Finish()
    {
        _finishing = true;
        _showing = true;
        _endIndex = 0;
        _text.text = TutorialScript.EndLines[0];
        _hint.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        _bubbleGroup.blocksRaycasts = true;
        _bubble.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        LayoutRebuilder.ForceRebuildLayoutImmediate(_bubble);
        Place();
    }

    private void Leave()
    {
        PlayerPrefs.SetInt(TutorialScript.DoneKey, 1);
        PlayerPrefs.Save();
        SceneManager.LoadScene("Main");
    }

    // ───────────────────────── 위치 ─────────────────────────

    private void LateUpdate()
    {
        if (_bubble.gameObject.activeSelf) Place();
    }

    private RectTransform AnchorRect(TutorialStep s)
    {
        if (_board == null) _board = FindFirstObjectByType<BoardPanel>();
        if (_battle == null) _battle = FindFirstObjectByType<BattleManagerTemp>();
        if (s == null || _board == null || _battle == null) return null;
        switch (s.anchor)
        {
            case TutorialAnchor.Waiting: return _battle.GetWaitingRect(s.unit);
            case TutorialAnchor.Cell: return _battle.GetCellRect(s.cell);
            case TutorialAnchor.Unit: return s.unit >= 0 ? _battle.GetUnitImage(s.unit).rectTransform : null;
            default: return _board.TutorialAnchorRect(s.anchor, s.unit);
        }
    }

    // 밝게 남길 영역: 시킨 행동에 필요한 곳만. 나머지는 어둡게 덮고 클릭도 막는다.
    private bool TryFocus(TutorialStep s, out Rect focus)
    {
        focus = default;
        if (s == null || _battle == null) return false;
        bool any = false;
        Rect area = default;   // out 매개변수는 지역 함수 안에서 쓸 수 없어 따로 모은다
        void Add(RectTransform rt)
        {
            if (rt == null || !rt.gameObject.activeInHierarchy || !TryScreenRect(rt, out var r)) return;
            if (!any) { area = r; any = true; }
            else area = Rect.MinMaxRect(Mathf.Min(area.xMin, r.xMin), Mathf.Min(area.yMin, r.yMin), Mathf.Max(area.xMax, r.xMax), Mathf.Max(area.yMax, r.yMax));
        }
        switch (s.act)
        {
            case TutorialAct.Select: Add(_battle.GetWaitingRect(s.unit)); break;
            case TutorialAct.Place: Add(_battle.GetCellRect(s.cell)); break;
            case TutorialAct.Move: Add(UnitCell(s.unit)); Add(_battle.GetCellRect(s.cell)); break;
            case TutorialAct.Judge: Add(UnitCell(s.unit)); break;
            default:
                if (s.anchor == TutorialAnchor.Center) break;
                Add(s.anchor == TutorialAnchor.Unit && s.unit >= 0 ? UnitCell(s.unit) : AnchorRect(s));
                if (s.cellTo.HasValue) Add(_battle.GetCellRect(s.cellTo.Value));
                break;
        }
        if (any) { area.xMin -= FocusPad; area.yMin -= FocusPad; area.xMax += FocusPad; area.yMax += FocusPad; }
        focus = area;
        return any;
    }

    // 캐릭터 그림은 칸보다 커서, 캐릭터가 선 칸으로 밝힌다
    private RectTransform UnitCell(int unit) => _battle.GetCellRect(_battle.GetUnitPos(unit));

    private void Place()
    {
        var s = _finishing ? null : Current;
        if (s != null) AnchorRect(s);   // _board, _battle 찾기
        Rect focus = default;
        bool hasFocus = !_finishing && TryFocus(s, out focus);
        LayoutDim(hasFocus ? focus : (Rect?)null);

        var size = _bubble.rect.size;
        var half = _root.rect.size * 0.5f;
        if (!hasFocus)
        {
            _bubble.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, -half.y + size.y * 0.5f + 140f);
            _arrow.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            return;
        }
        var r = focus;
        // 기본은 밝힌 곳 위. 화면 위가 모자라면 아래.
        bool above = r.yMax + Gap + size.y < half.y - 10f;
        float y = above ? r.yMax + Gap + size.y * 0.5f : r.yMin - Gap - size.y * 0.5f;
        float x = Mathf.Clamp(r.center.x, -half.x + size.x * 0.5f + 16f, half.x - size.x * 0.5f - 16f);
        y = Mathf.Clamp(y, -half.y + size.y * 0.5f + 16f, half.y - size.y * 0.5f - 16f);
        _bubble.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(x, y);

        _arrow.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        _arrow.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(Mathf.Clamp(r.center.x, x - size.x * 0.5f + 40f, x + size.x * 0.5f - 40f),
            above ? y - size.y * 0.5f - 10f : y + size.y * 0.5f + 10f);
        _arrow.localEulerAngles = new Vector3(0f, 0f, above ? 180f : 0f);
    }

    // 어두운 판 4장(위·아래·왼쪽·오른쪽)으로 구멍 하나를 남긴다. 구멍이 없으면 화면 전체를 덮는다.
    private void LayoutDim(Rect? hole)
    {
        var half = _root.rect.size * 0.5f;
        var screen = Rect.MinMaxRect(-half.x, -half.y, half.x, half.y);
        if (!hole.HasValue)
        {
            SetDim(0, screen);
            for (int i = 1; i < 4; i++) _dim[i].gameObject.SetActive(false);
            return;
        }
        var h = hole.Value;
        SetDim(0, Rect.MinMaxRect(screen.xMin, h.yMax, screen.xMax, screen.yMax));   // 위
        SetDim(1, Rect.MinMaxRect(screen.xMin, screen.yMin, screen.xMax, h.yMin));   // 아래
        SetDim(2, Rect.MinMaxRect(screen.xMin, h.yMin, h.xMin, h.yMax));             // 왼쪽
        SetDim(3, Rect.MinMaxRect(h.xMax, h.yMin, screen.xMax, h.yMax));             // 오른쪽
    }

    private void SetDim(int i, Rect r)
    {
        var d = _dim[i];
        bool show = r.width > 0.5f && r.height > 0.5f;
        d.gameObject.SetActive(show);
        if (!show) return;
        d.anchoredPosition = r.center;
        d.sizeDelta = r.size;
    }

    /// <summary>다른 캔버스의 RectTransform을 이 캔버스의 좌표(가운데 기준)로 바꾼다.</summary>
    private bool TryScreenRect(RectTransform target, out Rect rect)
    {
        rect = default;
        var canvas = target.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
        if (canvas == null) return false;
        canvas = canvas.rootCanvas;
        var cam = canvas.renderMode == RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay ? null : canvas.worldCamera;
        var corners = new Vector3[4];
        target.GetWorldCorners(corners);
        Vector2 min = new Vector2(float.MaxValue, float.MaxValue), max = new Vector2(float.MinValue, float.MinValue);
        foreach (var c in corners)
        {
            var screen = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(cam, c);
            if (!RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(_root, screen, null, out var local)) return false;
            min = Vector2.Min(min, local);
            max = Vector2.Max(max, local);
        }
        rect = new Rect(min, max - min);
        return true;
    }

    // ───────────────────────── 화면 만들기 ─────────────────────────

    private void BuildUi()
    {
        var canvas = gameObject.AddComponent<Canvas>();
        canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
        canvas.sortingOrder = 520;   // 전투 UI 위, ESC 메뉴(550) 아래
        var scaler = gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasScaler>();
        scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
        scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1920, 1080);
        scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = 0.5f;
        gameObject.AddComponent<GraphicRaycaster>();

        _root = NewRect("Root", transform);
        _root.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        _root.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        _root.offsetMin = _root.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;

        // 밝힌 곳 말고는 어둡게. 어두운 판은 클릭을 막아서 시킨 곳만 누를 수 있다.
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        {
            _dim[i] = NewRect("Dim" + i, _root);
            var dim = _dim[i].gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
            dim.color = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, DimAlpha);
            dim.raycastTarget = true;
        }

        var font = Resources.Load<TMP_FontAsset>("Fonts & Materials/BookkMyungjo_Light SDF");

        _bubble = NewRect("Bubble", _root);
        _bubble.sizeDelta = new Vector2(BubbleWidth, 0f);
        _bubbleGroup = _bubble.gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasGroup>();
        var fit = _bubble.gameObject.AddComponent<ContentSizeFitter>();
        fit.verticalFit = ContentSizeFitter.FitMode.PreferredSize;
        var layout = _bubble.gameObject.AddComponent<VerticalLayoutGroup>();
        layout.padding = new RectOffset(56, 56, 44, 40);
        layout.spacing = 6f;
        layout.childControlWidth = layout.childControlHeight = true;
        layout.childForceExpandHeight = false;

        // 어두운 바탕 (대사창 그림은 테두리만 있다)
        var back = _bubble.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        back.color = new Color(0.06f, 0.06f, 0.07f, 0.9f);
        var button = _bubble.gameObject.AddComponent<Button>();
        button.transition = Selectable.Transition.None;
        button.onClick.AddListener(OnBubbleClicked);

        var frame = NewRect("Frame", _bubble);
        frame.gameObject.AddComponent<LayoutElement>().ignoreLayout = true;
        frame.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        frame.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        frame.offsetMin = new Vector2(-10f, -10f);
        frame.offsetMax = new Vector2(10f, 10f);
        var frameImage = frame.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        var sprites = Resources.LoadAll<Sprite>("대사창");
        if (sprites.Length > 0) { frameImage.sprite = sprites[0]; frameImage.type = Image.Type.Sliced; }
        else frameImage.enabled = false;
        frameImage.raycastTarget = false;

        var name = NewText("Name", _bubble, font, 26f, new Color(1f, 0.84f, 0.48f));
        name.text = TutorialScript.BossName;
        _text = NewText("Text", _bubble, font, 32f, Color.white);
        _text.lineSpacing = 12f;
        _hint = NewText("Hint", _bubble, font, 22f, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.55f));
        _hint.text = "클릭해서 계속 ▶";
        _hint.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Right;

        _arrow = NewRect("Arrow", _root);
        _arrow.sizeDelta = new Vector2(36f, 22f);
        var arrow = _arrow.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        arrow.sprite = TriangleSprite();
        arrow.color = Color.white;
        arrow.raycastTarget = false;

        Hide();
    }

    private static RectTransform NewRect(string name, Transform parent)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform));
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        return rect;
    }

    private static TextMeshProUGUI NewText(string name, Transform parent, TMP_FontAsset font, float size, Color color)
    {
        var text = NewRect(name, parent).gameObject.AddComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
        if (font != null) text.font = font;
        text.fontSize = size;
        text.color = color;
        text.raycastTarget = false;
        text.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;
        return text;
    }

    // 위를 가리키는 작은 삼각형 (회전해서 아래도 가리킨다)
    private static Sprite TriangleSprite()
    {
        const int w = 36, h = 22;
        var tex = new Texture2D(w, h, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false) { filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear };
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x++)
        {
            float half = (float)y / (h - 1) * (w * 0.5f);
            bool inside = Mathf.Abs(x + 0.5f - w * 0.5f) <= (w * 0.5f - half);
            tex.SetPixel(x, y, inside ? Color.white : Color.clear);
        }
        tex.Apply();
        return Sprite.Create(tex, new Rect(0, 0, w, h), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f));
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/Tutorial/TutorialNpc.cs
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

/// <summary>
/// 마을 가운데 화면(Main Panel) 왼쪽의 튜토리얼 NPC. 클릭하면 튜토리얼을 다시 할지 묻는다. (보상 없음)
/// 그림: Resources/마을_튜토리얼NPC_흰 (다른 NPC처럼 흰 실루엣. 마을_튜토리얼NPC.png 시안에서 잘라 냄)
/// 색 그림이 생기면 NpcHoverEffect.ColorSheets의 "마을_튜토리얼NPC_흰" 항목에 이름을 넣으면 된다.
/// </summary>
public static class TutorialNpc
{
    // 시안(1920×1080)에서 실루엣이 차지하는 영역 (Unity 기준: 아래가 0)
    private static readonly Vector2 AnchorMin = new Vector2(228f / 1920f, 1f - 672f / 1080f);
    private static readonly Vector2 AnchorMax = new Vector2(483f / 1920f, 1f - 264f / 1080f);

    /// <summary>MainManager.Start에서 NpcHoverEffect.Setup 전에 부른다.</summary>
    public static void Create(GameObject village)
    {
        Transform parent = null;
        foreach (var t in village.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true))
            if (t.name == "Main Panel") { parent = t.Find("Button Panel1") ?? t; break; }
        var sprite = Resources.Load<Sprite>("마을_튜토리얼NPC_흰");
        if (parent == null || sprite == null)
        {
            Debug.LogWarning("[TutorialNpc] 마을 가운데 화면 또는 NPC 그림을 찾지 못했습니다.");
            return;
        }

        var go = new GameObject("Tutorial NPC Button", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image), typeof(Button));
        go.layer = parent.gameObject.layer;
        var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
        rect.SetParent(parent, false);
        rect.anchorMin = AnchorMin;
        rect.anchorMax = AnchorMax;
        rect.offsetMin = rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
        image.sprite = sprite;
        image.preserveAspect = true;
        go.GetComponent<Button>().onClick.AddListener(Ask);
    }

    private static void Ask()
    {
        ModalDialog.Show("튜토리얼",
            "튜토리얼을 다시 해 볼까요?",
            "기사·궁수·도적으로 연습해요. 보상은 없어요.",
            null,
            new ModalDialog.Option("시작", TutorialLink.Begin),
            new ModalDialog.Option("돌아가기", null));
    }
}

FILE Assets/JSW/Script/Tutorial/TutorialScript.cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

public enum TutorialAct { Next, Select, Place, Drag, Move, Judge }
public enum TutorialAnchor { Center, Waiting, Cell, Unit, Portrait, ActionPoints, WillPower, PlayerHp, Item }

/// <summary>튜토리얼 한 단계. 말풍선이 anchor를 가리키고, act를 해야 다음 단계로 넘어간다.</summary>
public sealed class TutorialStep
{
    public int turn;            // 몇 턴에 나오는가
    public bool place;          // true면 배치 단계, false면 행동 단계
    public TutorialAct act;
    public int unit = -1;       // 0 기사, 1 궁수, 2 도적 (편성 순서)
    public Vector2Int cell;     // 배치·이동할 칸
    public Vector2Int? cellTo;  // 설명할 칸이 여러 개면 cell~cellTo 사각형을 밝힌다
    public TutorialAnchor anchor;
    public string text;
}

/// <summary>
/// 튜토리얼 대본 (2026-11-03, 사용자 대사 흐름 반영). 무작위 없음. 좌표는 (가로, 세로), (0,0)이 판 왼쪽 위.
/// 편성: 기사, 궁수, 도적 + 소금물. 안내자 HP 43 = 기사 3 + 궁수 11 + 기사 5 + 도적 24.
/// 공격 칸(BellkeeperTrial.Shape): 1·4·5턴 가로줄(세로 4, 가로 2~6) / 2턴 세로줄(가로 3, 세로 2~6) / 3턴 가운데 3×3(가로 3~5, 세로 2~4).
/// 피격: 2턴 기사(HP -3, 힘 +2), 3턴 도적(욕심 칸 버팀, HP -4, 힘 +9). 의지력 1턴 0, 이후 1. 소금물은 4턴에만 HP +1.
/// 턴이 끝난 뒤 이어지는 대사(같은 턴의 Next)가 남아 있으면 보스 공격을 미룬다 (TutorialDirector.HoldsTurnEnd).
/// </summary>
public static class TutorialScript
{
    public const string BossId = "tutorial";
    public const string BossName = "안내자";
    public const int BossHp = 43;
    public const string ItemId = "4";   // 소금물
    public static readonly int[] UnitIds = { 1, 2, 3 };   // 기사, 궁수, 도적
    public const string DoneKey = "Tutorial.Done";

    public static int ActionPoints(int turn) => turn switch { 1 => 4, 3 => 2, _ => 1 };
    public static int WillPower(int turn) => turn <= 1 ? 0 : 1;

    /// <summary>턴별 힘 칸. true = 욕심 칸.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<(Vector2Int cell, bool greed)> PowerCells(int turn)
    {
        switch (turn)
        {
            case 1: yield return (new Vector2Int(3, 5), false); yield return (new Vector2Int(4, 5), false); break;
            case 3: yield return (new Vector2Int(4, 3), true); yield return (new Vector2Int(3, 6), false); break;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>안내자를 쓰러뜨린 뒤 차례로 보여 주는 대사. 마지막 대사를 클릭하면 마을로.</summary>
    public static readonly string[] EndLines =
    {
        "커허억!!",
        "……허어? 요것 봐라?",
        "제법 야무지게 때리는구먼……!",
        "크흐흐…… 아따, 제대로 한 방 먹었네.",
        "거참, 꼬맹이한테 얻어맞고 뻗을 줄이야!",
        "허허허! 이거 참, 기분 한번 더럽게 좋구먼!",
        "좋아, 좋아! 이만하면 됐어!",
        "이제 가서 다른 놈들도 실컷 두들겨 패 보라고!",
        "크하하! 다음에 만나면 나도 제대로 상대해 줄 테니께!",
    };

    // 해야 할 행동 글자 강조
    private const string H = "<color=#FFD27A>", E = "</color>";

    private static TutorialStep P(TutorialAct act, int unit, int x, int y, TutorialAnchor anchor, string text)
        => new TutorialStep { turn = 1, place = true, act = act, unit = unit, cell = new Vector2Int(x, y), anchor = anchor, text = text };
    private static TutorialStep S(int turn, TutorialAct act, int unit, int x, int y, TutorialAnchor anchor, string text)
        => new TutorialStep { turn = turn, act = act, unit = unit, cell = new Vector2Int(x, y), anchor = anchor, text = text };
    private static TutorialStep Area(int turn, int x, int y, int x2, int y2, string text)
    {
        var s = S(turn, TutorialAct.Next, -1, x, y, TutorialAnchor.Cell, text);
        s.cellTo = new Vector2Int(x2, y2);
        return s;
    }
    private static TutorialStep PSay(string text)
        => P(TutorialAct.Next, -1, 0, 0, TutorialAnchor.Center, text);
    private static TutorialStep Say(int turn, TutorialAnchor anchor, string text, int unit = -1, int x = 0, int y = 0)
        => S(turn, TutorialAct.Next, unit, x, y, anchor, text);

    public static readonly TutorialStep[] Steps =
    {

    /// <summary>안내자 보스 데이터. 에셋 없이 코드로 만든다. 배경음은 다른 보스 것을 빌린다.</summary>
    public static BossTemp CreateBoss(BossTemp musicFrom)
    {
        var boss = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<BossTemp>();
        boss.name = "Tutorial";
        boss.bossId = BossId;
        boss.bossName = BossName;
        boss.maxHp = BossHp;
        boss.bossSprite = Resources.Load<Sprite>("튜토리얼_보스");
        if (boss.bossSprite == null)   // 가져오기 설정이 Multiple이면 첫 조각을 쓴다
        {
            var parts = Resources.LoadAll<Sprite>("튜토리얼_보스");
            if (parts.Length > 0) boss.bossSprite = parts[0];
        }
        boss.battleStart = "";
        boss.win = "";
        if (musicFrom != null) { boss.bgmStart = musicFrom.bgmStart; boss.bgmLoop = musicFrom.bgmLoop; }
        return boss;
    }
}

/// <summary>튜토리얼 전투로 들어가는 길. 오프닝(새 게임)과 마을 NPC에서 부른다.</summary>
public static class TutorialLink
{
    private static bool _pending;

    public static void Begin()
    {
        _pending = true;
        UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.LoadScene("BattleTemp");
    }

    public static bool Consume()
    {
        bool p = _pending;
        _pending = false;
        return p;
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/Card.cs
using System;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class Card : MonoBehaviour
{
    public UnitTemp unitTemp;
    public bool isSelected = false;
    [SerializeField] private Image image;
    
    public TextMeshProUGUI text;
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI popupText;
    [SerializeField] private GameObject popUpPanel;
    public GameObject border;

    [Header("잠금 (JSW)")]
    [Tooltip("잠긴 캐릭터 카드 위에 덮는 그림 (JSW/UI/Formation/카드_잠금.png)")]
    [SerializeField] private GameObject lockCover;
    [NonSerialized] public bool locked;
    [Tooltip("글자 띠 바탕 (Text 앞 형제). 글자 줄 수에 맞춰 높이가 바뀐다")]
    [SerializeField] private RectTransform labelBack;

    public void Init()
    {
        popUpPanel.SetActive(false);
        popupText.text = 
            $"<align=\"center\"><size=45>{unitTemp.unitName}</size></align>\n" +
            $"<align=\"left\"><size=30>" +
            $"힘: {unitTemp.power}\n" +
            $"지능: {unitTemp.intelligence}\n" +
            $"부활 대기 시간: {unitTemp.reviveCool}턴\n" +
            $"능력: {unitTemp.abilityText}" +
            $"</size>";
        image.sprite = unitTemp.illustration;
        // 잠긴 캐릭터는 일러스트를 보여 주지 않는다 (해금하는 재미)
        image.gameObject.SetActive(!locked);
        if (lockCover != null) lockCover.SetActive(locked);
        text.gameObject.SetActive(isSelected && !locked);
        if (labelBack != null) labelBack.gameObject.SetActive(text.gameObject.activeSelf);
    }

    /// <summary>카드 아래쪽 글자 띠. 빈 문자열이면 숨긴다.</summary>
    public void SetLabel(string label)
    {
        bool show = !locked && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(label);
        text.text = show ? label : "";
        text.gameObject.SetActive(show);
        if (labelBack == null) return;
        labelBack.gameObject.SetActive(show);
        int lines = show ? label.Split('\n').Length : 1;
        float h = 18f + 32f * lines;
        labelBack.sizeDelta = new Vector2(labelBack.sizeDelta.x, h);
        // 글자 칸도 바탕과 같은 위치·높이로 (한 줄일 때 위로 뜨지 않게)
        var tr = text.rectTransform;
        tr.anchoredPosition = labelBack.anchoredPosition;
        tr.sizeDelta = new Vector2(tr.sizeDelta.x, h);
    }

    public void OnMouseEnter()
    {
        if (locked) return;
        popUpPanel.SetActive(true);
    }

    public void OnMouseExit()
    {
        popUpPanel.SetActive(false);
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/CharacterPassiveState.cs
/// <summary>
/// 한 전투에서만 유지되는 캐릭터 패시브 상태. 원본 UnitTemp를 변경하지 않는다.
/// </summary>
public sealed class CharacterPassiveState
{
    private readonly int characterId;
    private bool moved;
    private bool shieldAvailable = true;

    public int PowerBonus { get; private set; }
    public int IntelligenceBonus { get; private set; }
    public int MoveDamage => characterId == 3 ? 3 : 0;
    public int MoveHealing => characterId == 5 ? 1 : 0;
    public int TurnEndHealing => characterId == 4 ? 3 : 0;
    public int TurnEndDamage => characterId == 2 && !moved ? 5 : 0;
    public bool LosesBattleOnDeath => characterId == 9;

    public CharacterPassiveState(int characterId)
    {
        this.characterId = characterId;
    }

    public void StartTurn()
    {
        moved = false;
        IntelligenceBonus = 0;
    }

    public void Move() => moved = true;

    public void AfterJudge()
    {
        // Wizard's base intelligence is 4; judgment can raise it to 6 this turn.
        if (characterId == 7 && IntelligenceBonus < 2) IntelligenceBonus++;
    }

    public bool TryBlockHit()
    {
        if (characterId != 1 || !shieldAvailable) return false;
        shieldAvailable = false;
        return true;
    }

    public void OnDeath()
    {
        shieldAvailable = true;
    }

    public void OnAllyDeath()
    {
        if (characterId == 6) PowerBonus++;
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/FormationSave.cs
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEngine;

// Save IDs rather than scene references. Empty item slots are saved explicitly.
public static class FormationSave
{
    private const string Prefix = "Formation_v1_";

    public static void Save(UnitTemp[] units, ItemData[] items, IEnumerable<UnitTemp> roster)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < units.Length; i++)
            PlayerPrefs.SetInt(Prefix + "Unit_" + i, units[i] != null ? units[i].id : 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++)
            PlayerPrefs.SetString(Prefix + "Item_" + i, items[i] != null ? items[i].id : "");
        foreach (var unit in roster.Where(u => u != null).Distinct())
            PlayerPrefs.SetInt(Prefix + "Skin_" + unit.id, unit.IsSkinUnlocked(unit.currentSkin) ? unit.currentSkin : 0);
        PlayerPrefs.Save();
    }

    public static void Load(UnitTemp[] units, ItemData[] items, IEnumerable<UnitTemp> roster, IEnumerable<ItemData> catalog)
    {
        var available = roster.Where(u => u != null).Distinct().ToArray();
        var usedUnits = new HashSet<int>();
        // Resolve all saved slots first so a missing character cannot displace another valid slot.
        for (int i = 0; i < units.Length; i++)
        {
            int id = PlayerPrefs.GetInt(Prefix + "Unit_" + i, units[i] != null ? units[i].id : 0);
            units[i] = available.FirstOrDefault(u => u.id == id && OwnsUnit(u.id) && !usedUnits.Contains(u.id));
            if (units[i] != null) usedUnits.Add(units[i].id);
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < units.Length; i++)
        {
            if (units[i] != null) continue;
            units[i] = available.FirstOrDefault(u => OwnsUnit(u.id) && !usedUnits.Contains(u.id));
            if (units[i] != null) usedUnits.Add(units[i].id);
        }
        foreach (var unit in available)
        {
            int skin = PlayerPrefs.GetInt(Prefix + "Skin_" + unit.id, 0);
            unit.currentSkin = unit.IsSkinUnlocked(skin) ? skin : 0;
        }
        var usedItems = new HashSet<string>();
        for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++)
        {
            string id = PlayerPrefs.GetString(Prefix + "Item_" + i, items[i] != null ? items[i].id : "");
            items[i] = catalog.FirstOrDefault(item => item != null && item.id == id
                && PlayerPrefs.GetInt("Item_" + id, 0) > 0 && !usedItems.Contains(id));
            if (items[i] != null) usedItems.Add(items[i].id);
        }
    }

    private static bool OwnsUnit(int id) => (id >= 1 && id <= 3) || PlayerPrefs.GetInt("Character_" + id, 0) == 1;
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/ItemBattleState.cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

/// <summary>Equipped item rules and battle-local limits. Never modifies item/unit assets.</summary>
public sealed class ItemBattleState
{
    private readonly HashSet<string> equipped;
    private bool wineUsed;
    private bool contractUsed;

    public ItemBattleState(IEnumerable<string> ids)
    {
        equipped = new HashSet<string>(ids ?? Array.Empty<string>());
    }

    private int Bonus(string id, int amount) => equipped.Contains(id) ? amount : 0;
    public int PowerBonus(int characterId, int hp) => Bonus("1", 2) + Bonus("15", 1)
        + Bonus("19", 2) + ConditionalBonus(characterId, hp);
    public int IntelligenceBonus(int characterId, int hp) => Bonus("7", 2) + Bonus("14", 1)
        + Bonus("19", 2) + ConditionalBonus(characterId, hp);
    private int ConditionalBonus(int characterId, int hp) => (hp <= 5 ? Bonus("13", 5) : 0)
        + (characterId == 9 ? Bonus("22", 3) : 0);

    public int MoveDamage => Bonus("3", 1);
    public int DeathDamage => Bonus("5", 5);
    public int DeathHealing => Bonus("6", 3);
    public int TurnHealing => Bonus("4", 2) + Bonus("20", 1);
    public int TurnHpCost => Bonus("16", 2);
    public int ActionPointBonus => Bonus("16", 1);
    public int WillPowerReduction => Bonus("21", 1);
    public int ReviveTurns(int original) => Math.Max(0, original - Bonus("9", 1));
    public int TurnDamage(Func<int, int, int> randomRange) => Bonus("2", 3)
        + (equipped.Contains("12") ? randomRange(1, 7) : 0);

    public void StartTurn() => wineUsed = false;

    // Resolve refunds before the caller advances the turn at zero action points.
    public int SpendAction(int current, bool judgment, Func<int, int, int> randomRange)
    {
        int remaining = Math.Max(0, current - 1);
        if (judgment && equipped.Contains("8") && randomRange(0, 100) < 33) remaining++;
        if (remaining == 0 && equipped.Contains("11") && !wineUsed)
        {
            wineUsed = true;
            remaining = 1;
        }
        return remaining;
    }

    public int ResolveHp(int hp, int maxHp)
    {
        if (hp <= 0 && equipped.Contains("23") && !contractUsed)
        {
            contractUsed = true;
            hp = 10;
        }
        return Math.Max(0, Math.Min(maxHp, hp));
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/ItemBuildingPanel.cs
using System;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class ItemBuildingPanel : MonoBehaviour
{

    [Serializable]
    private struct ItemSlot
    {
        public GameObject gameObject;
        public TextMeshProUGUI name;
        public TextMeshProUGUI description;
        public Image image;
        public int itemId;
    }

    [SerializeField] private ItemUI[] items;
    [SerializeField] private ItemSlot[] slots = new ItemSlot[3];
    [SerializeField] private GameObject itemBoard;

    private void OnEnable()
    {
        Init();
    }

    public void Init()
    {
        var display = BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance();
        display.EnsureFormationLoaded();
        for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++)
        {
            items[i].itemData = ItemManager.Instance.GetItemData(items[i].id.ToString());
            items[i].isEquip = Array.Exists(display.currentItems, item => item != null && item == items[i].itemData);
            items[i].Init();
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
        {
            var item = display.currentItems[i];
            bool equipped = item != null;
            slots[i].itemId = equipped ? int.Parse(item.id) : 0;
            slots[i].name.text = equipped ? item.itemName : "";
            slots[i].description.text = equipped ? item.description : "";
            slots[i].image.sprite = equipped ? item.icon : null;
            slots[i].name.gameObject.SetActive(equipped);
            slots[i].description.gameObject.SetActive(equipped);
            slots[i].image.gameObject.SetActive(equipped);
        }
    }

    [ContextMenu("Setting")]
    public void Setting()
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++)
        {
            items[i].id = i+1;
            items[i].popup = items[i].gameObject.transform.GetChild(0).gameObject;
            items[i].nameText = items[i].popup.GetComponentsInChildren<TextMeshProUGUI>()[0];
            items[i].descriptionText = items[i].popup.GetComponentsInChildren<TextMeshProUGUI>()[1];
            items[i].image = items[i].gameObject.GetComponent<Image>();
        }
    }

    public void OnItemClick(int id)
    {
        if (id < 1 || id > items.Length || !items[id - 1].unlock) return;
        var item = items[id - 1];
        // 여정 중 정비 제한 (JSW/Script/JourneyLink.cs)
        if (JourneyRefit.Limited)
        {
            var display = BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance();
            int slot = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
                if (item.isEquip ? slots[i].itemId == id : slots[i].name.text == "") { slot = i; break; }
            if (slot >= 0 && !JourneyRefit.CanSetItem(display.currentUnits, display.currentItems, slot, item.isEquip ? null : item.itemData)) return;
        }
        if (!item.isEquip)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                var slot = slots[i];
                if(slot.name.text != "") continue;
                slot.name.text = item.itemData.itemName;
                slot.description.text = item.itemData.description;
                slot.image.sprite = item.itemData.icon;
                slots[i].itemId = id;
                slot.name.gameObject.SetActive(true);
                slot.image.gameObject.SetActive(true);
                slot.description.gameObject.SetActive(true);
                BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentItems[i] = item.itemData;
                items[id - 1].isEquip = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                var slot = slots[i];
                if (slot.itemId != id) continue;
                slots[i].itemId = 0;
                slot.name.text = "";
                slot.name.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                slot.description.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                slot.image.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentItems[i] = null;
                items[id - 1].isEquip = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().SaveFormation();
        JourneyRefit.Refresh(BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentUnits, BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentItems);
    }

    public void UnEquipItemInSlot(int i)
    {
        if (i < 0 || i >= slots.Length) return;
        if(!slots[i].name.gameObject.activeSelf) return;
        if(!items[slots[i].itemId - 1].isEquip) return;
        var refitDisplay = BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance();
        if (!JourneyRefit.CanSetItem(refitDisplay.currentUnits, refitDisplay.currentItems, i, null)) return;
        items[slots[i].itemId - 1].isEquip = false;
        slots[i].name.text = "";
        slots[i].name.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        slots[i].description.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        slots[i].image.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentItems[i] = null;
        slots[i].itemId = 0;
        BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().SaveFormation();
        JourneyRefit.Refresh(refitDisplay.currentUnits, refitDisplay.currentItems);
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/ItemUI.cs
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using Random = UnityEngine.Random;

public class ItemUI : MonoBehaviour
{
    public int id;
    public ItemData itemData;
    public GameObject popup;
    public TextMeshProUGUI nameText;
    public TextMeshProUGUI descriptionText;
    public Image image;
    public bool unlock;
    public bool isEquip;

    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI talkText;

    [Header("잠금 (JSW)")]
    [Tooltip("얻지 않은 아이템 위에 덮는 반투명 자물쇠 (JSW/UI/Formation/아이템_잠금.png)")]
    [SerializeField] private GameObject lockCover;

    public void Init()
    {
        StopAllCoroutines();
        if (talkText != null) talkText.text = "";
        if (popup != null) popup.SetActive(false);
        if (itemData is null)
        {
            id = 0;
            unlock = false;
            image.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            return;
        }
        id = int.Parse(itemData.id);
        unlock = InventoryManager.Instance.HasItem(id.ToString());
        nameText.text = itemData.itemName;
        descriptionText.text = itemData.description;
        image.sprite = itemData.icon;
        // 얻지 않은 아이템도 판에 보여 주고, 자물쇠를 덮는다 (자물쇠 뒤로 아이템이 비침)
        if (!unlock) descriptionText.text += "\n<color=#9A9A9A><size=85%>아직 얻지 못했어요</size></color>";
        image.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        gameObject.SetActive(true);
        if (lockCover != null) lockCover.SetActive(!unlock);
    }

    public void OnMouseEnter()
    {
        if (itemData == null) return;
        popup.SetActive(true);
    }

    public void OnMouseExit()
    {
        if (itemData == null) return;
        popup.SetActive(false);
    }

    public void OnTurnStart()
    {
        if (itemData == null || !unlock || !isActiveAndEnabled) return;
        if(id is not (10 or 17 or 18)) return;
        if (talkText == null)
        {
            var bubble = new GameObject("ItemDialogue", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(TextMeshProUGUI));
            bubble.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            talkText = bubble.GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
            talkText.font = nameText.font;
            talkText.fontSize = 20;
            talkText.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            talkText.raycastTarget = false;
            var rect = talkText.rectTransform;
            rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 1f);
            rect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0f);
            rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 8f);
            rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(240f, 70f);
        }
        List<string> dialogue = new List<string>();
        switch (id)
        {
            case 10:
                dialogue = _dialogue[0].ToList();
                break;
            case 17:
                dialogue = _dialogue[1].ToList();
                break;
            case 18:
                dialogue = _dialogue[2].ToList();
                break;
        }

        dialogue.Shuffle();
        StopAllCoroutines();
        StartCoroutine(PrintText(dialogue[0]));
    }

    IEnumerator PrintText(string text)
    {
        talkText.text = "";
        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        
        foreach (var t in text)
        {
            sb.Append(t);
            talkText.text = sb.ToString();
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.1f);
        }
        
        yield return new WaitForSeconds(3f);
        talkText.text = "";
    }

    private string[][] _dialogue =
    {
        new string[]
        {
            "아직 살아 있지? 나도 그래!",
            "이번엔 어디로 갈 거야?",
            "내 얘기 좀 들어 봐!"
        },
        new string[]
        {
            "살랑, 살랑.",
            "바람이 불어오네.",
            "오늘도 꽃잎을 흔들어.",
        },
        new string[]
        {
            "흐음~ 흠흠~♪",
            "라라라~♪",
            "이 노래, 기억하니?",
        }
    };
}

using System;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class Unit : MonoBehaviour
{
    public UnitTemp unitTemp;
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI powerText;
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI intelligenceText;
    [SerializeField] private Image sd;
    
    public void Init()
    {
        powerText.text = $"<color=#FFB080>힘 {unitTemp.power}</color>";
        intelligenceText.text = $"<color=#88CEFF>지능 {unitTemp.intelligence}</color>";
        if (!powerText.enableAutoSizing) powerText.fontSizeMax = powerText.fontSize;
        if (!intelligenceText.enableAutoSizing) intelligenceText.fontSizeMax = intelligenceText.fontSize;
        powerText.fontSizeMin = intelligenceText.fontSizeMin = 18;
        powerText.enableAutoSizing = intelligenceText.enableAutoSizing = true;
        powerText.textWrappingMode = intelligenceText.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
        if (!unitTemp.IsSkinUnlocked(unitTemp.currentSkin)) unitTemp.currentSkin = 0;
        sd.sprite = unitTemp.currentSkin switch
        {
            0 => unitTemp.defaultSkin,
            1 => unitTemp.skin1,
            2 => unitTemp.skin2,
            3 => unitTemp.skin3,
            _ => sd.sprite
        };
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/UnitBuildingPanel.cs
using System;
using System.Linq;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class UnitBuildingPanel : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Units")] public UnitTemp[] currentUnits = new UnitTemp[3];
    [SerializeField] private Unit[] unitObject = new Unit[3];

    [Header("UI")] [SerializeField] private GameObject formPanel;

    [SerializeField] private GameObject popup1;

    [SerializeField] private GameObject popup2;
    [SerializeField] private Unit popupUnit;
    [SerializeField] private GameObject arrow;
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI nameText;
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI descriptionText;

    [Space] [SerializeField] private GameObject changePanel;
    public Card[] cards = new Card[9];

    public Image[] skins = new Image[3];
    public TextMeshProUGUI[] skinTexts = new TextMeshProUGUI[3];
    [Tooltip("스킨 글자 바탕 (JSW)")] public GameObject[] skinLabelBacks = new GameObject[3];

    private int _currentUnitIndex = 0;
    private int _currentCardIndex = 0;
    private int _cardIndex = 0;

    private void OnEnable()
    {
        BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().EnsureFormationLoaded();
        currentUnits = BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentUnits;
        Init();
    }

    private void Init()
    {
        unitObject[0].unitTemp = currentUnits[0];
        unitObject[1].unitTemp = currentUnits[1];
        unitObject[2].unitTemp = currentUnits[2];

        unitObject[0].Init();
        unitObject[1].Init();
        unitObject[2].Init();
        if(!InventoryManager.Instance.HasCharacter("1")) InventoryManager.Instance.UnlockCharacter("1");
        if(!InventoryManager.Instance.HasCharacter("2")) InventoryManager.Instance.UnlockCharacter("2");
        if(!InventoryManager.Instance.HasCharacter("3")) InventoryManager.Instance.UnlockCharacter("3");
        BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentUnits = currentUnits;
    }

    public void OnMouseClick(int i)
    {
        popup2.SetActive(false);
        if (i == -1)
        {
            popup1.SetActive(false);
            return;
        }

        popup1.SetActive(true);
        var vector3 = popup1.transform.position;
        vector3.x = unitObject[i].transform.position.x;
        popup1.transform.position = vector3;
        _currentUnitIndex = i;
    }

    public void ShowInfo()
    {
        popup1.SetActive(false);
        popup2.SetActive(true);
        var vector3 = arrow.transform.position;
        vector3.x = unitObject[_currentUnitIndex].transform.position.x;
        arrow.transform.position = vector3;
        popupUnit.unitTemp = currentUnits[_currentUnitIndex];
        popupUnit.Init();

        nameText.text = popupUnit.unitTemp.unitName;
        descriptionText.text = popupUnit.unitTemp.abilityText;
    }

    public void OpenChangePanel()
    {
        formPanel.SetActive(false);
        changePanel.SetActive(true);

        var id = 0;
        foreach (var card in cards)
        {
            id++;
            card.locked = !InventoryManager.Instance.HasCharacter(id.ToString());
            card.isSelected = currentUnits.Contains(card.unitTemp);
            card.Init();
            card.border.SetActive(false);
            card.SetLabel(card.isSelected ? LabelEquipped : "");

            if (card.unitTemp == currentUnits[_currentUnitIndex])
            {
                _currentCardIndex = cards.ToList().IndexOf(card);
                _cardIndex = _currentCardIndex;
                card.border.SetActive(true);
                card.SetLabel(LabelCurrent);
            }
        }

        InitSkin();
    }

    // 카드 아래쪽 글자 띠 문구 (JSW)
    private const string LabelEquipped = "장착됨";
    private const string LabelCurrent = "현재 캐릭터\n눌러서 완료";
    private const string LabelSwap = "한 번 더 눌러\n교체";

    /// <summary>교체 화면의 '돌아가기': 바꾸지 않고 캐릭터 3명 화면으로 (JSW)</summary>
    public void CloseChangePanel()
    {
        popup1.SetActive(false);
        popup2.SetActive(false);
        changePanel.SetActive(false);
        formPanel.SetActive(true);
    }

    public void OnCardClick(int i)
    {
        if (cards[i].locked) return;
        if (_currentCardIndex == i)
        {
            popup1.SetActive(false);
            popup2.SetActive(false);

            formPanel.SetActive(true);
            changePanel.SetActive(false);

            // 여정 중 정비 제한 (JSW/Script/JourneyLink.cs)
            if (!JourneyRefit.CanSetUnit(currentUnits, BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentItems, _currentUnitIndex, cards[i].unitTemp)) return;
            currentUnits[_currentUnitIndex] = cards[i].unitTemp;

            Init();
            BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().SaveFormation();
            JourneyRefit.Refresh(currentUnits, BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().currentItems);
            return;
        }

        if (cards[i].isSelected && i != _cardIndex) return;

        var prev = cards[_currentCardIndex];
        prev.border.SetActive(false);
        prev.SetLabel(prev.isSelected ? LabelEquipped : "");

        _currentCardIndex = i;
        cards[i].border.SetActive(true);
        cards[i].SetLabel(i == _cardIndex ? LabelCurrent : LabelSwap);

        InitSkin();
    }

    private void InitSkin()
    {
        var unit = cards[_currentCardIndex].unitTemp;
        if (!unit.IsSkinUnlocked(unit.currentSkin)) unit.currentSkin = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < skins.Length; i++)
        {
            var sprite = unit.GetSkin(i + 1);
            bool unlocked = unit.IsSkinUnlocked(i + 1);
            skins[i].sprite = sprite;
            skins[i].gameObject.SetActive(sprite != null);
            // 잠긴 스킨은 어둡게 (JSW)
            skins[i].color = unlocked ? Color.white : new Color(0.22f, 0.22f, 0.22f, 1f);
            skinTexts[i].gameObject.SetActive(sprite != null);
            if (i < skinLabelBacks.Length && skinLabelBacks[i] != null) skinLabelBacks[i].SetActive(sprite != null);
            string label = unit.currentSkin == i + 1 ? "장착됨" : unlocked ? "해금됨" : "잠김";
            skinTexts[i].text = label;
        }
    }

    public void OnSkinClick(int i)
    {
        if (i < 1 || i > 3 || !cards[_currentCardIndex].unitTemp.IsSkinUnlocked(i)) return;
        if(cards[_currentCardIndex].unitTemp.currentSkin == i) cards[_currentCardIndex].unitTemp.currentSkin = 0;
        else cards[_currentCardIndex].unitTemp.currentSkin = i;
        BattleDisplayManager.GetInstance().SaveFormation();
        InitSkin();
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Building/UnitTemp.cs
using UnityEngine;

[CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "Unit", menuName = "Units/Unit")]
public class UnitTemp : ScriptableObject
{
    public string unitName;
    public int id;
    public int power;
    public int intelligence;
    public int reviveCool;
    public string abilityText;
    public Sprite illustration;
    public Sprite defaultSkin;
    public Sprite skin1;
    public Sprite skin2;
    public Sprite skin3;
    public bool isSkin1Unlocked = false;
    public bool isSkin2Unlocked = false;
    public bool isSkin3Unlocked = false;
    public int currentSkin = 0;

    public Sprite GetSkin(int index) => index switch
    {
        1 => skin1,
        2 => skin2,
        3 => skin3,
        _ => defaultSkin
    };

    public bool IsSkinUnlocked(int index)
    {
        if (index == 0) return true;
        if (index < 1 || index > 3 || GetSkin(index) == null) return false;
        // Existing UnitTemp assets store insect, boss, NPC (different from the shop enum).
        var skin = index switch { 1 => Skin.InsectSkin, 2 => Skin.BossSkin, _ => Skin.NpcSkin };
        return InventoryManager.Instance != null
            && InventoryManager.Instance.HasSkin(id.ToString(), skin);
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/CutScene/CutScenePanel.cs
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class CutScenePanel: MonoBehaviour
{
    public CutScene[] cutscenes;
    [HideInInspector] public CutScene currentCutScene;
    [SerializeField] private Image image;
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI text;
    [SerializeField] private Slider slider;

    [SerializeField] private float wordDelay = 0.1f;
    private float _wordDelay;
    [SerializeField] private float textDelay = 0.5f;
    [SerializeField] private float cutDelay = 0.5f;
    [SerializeField] private float timeForSkip = 2f;
    [SerializeField] private Button nextButton;
    [SerializeField] private TextFadeEffect textFadeEffect;
    private float _holdTime = 0f;
    private bool _isSkip = false;

    private int _currentCut = 0;
    private int _currentText = -1;

    public void SetCutScene(string cutsceneName)
    {
        _currentText = -1;
        slider.value = 0f;
        currentCutScene = null;
        currentCutScene = cutscenes.FirstOrDefault(x => x.cutSceneName == cutsceneName);
        if (currentCutScene == null)
        {
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
            return;
        }
        
        slider.gameObject.SetActive(currentCutScene.skippable);
        _holdTime = 0f;
        _isSkip = false;
        //StartCoroutine(PlayCutScene());
        NextText();
    }

    IEnumerator PlayCutScene()
    {
        foreach (var cut in currentCutScene.cuts)
        {
            image.sprite = cut.image;
            foreach (var t in cut.texts)
            {
                text.text = "";
                _wordDelay = wordDelay;
                foreach (var c in t)
                {
                    text.text += c;
                    yield return new WaitForSeconds(_wordDelay);
                }
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(textDelay);
            }
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(cutDelay);
        }

        if (currentCutScene.name is "Ending1" or "Ending2")
        {
            SceneManager.LoadScene("Main");
        }
        gameObject.SetActive(false);
    }

    public void NextText()
    {
        nextButton.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        _currentText++;
        if (currentCutScene.cuts[_currentCut].texts.Length <= _currentText)
        {
            _currentCut++;
            _currentText = 0;
        }

        if (currentCutScene.cuts.Length <= _currentCut)
        {
            if (currentCutScene.name is "Ending1" or "Ending2")
            {
                SceneManager.LoadScene("Main");
            }
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
            return;
        }
        
        image.sprite = currentCutScene.cuts[_currentCut].image;
        
        text.text = currentCutScene.cuts[_currentCut].texts[_currentText];
        textFadeEffect.FadeInText(text, () => nextButton.gameObject.SetActive(true));
    }

    public void ClickButton()
    {
        textFadeEffect.FadeOutText(text, () => NextText());
    }
    

    private void Update()
    {
        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Space))
        {
            _wordDelay = 0f;
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyUp(KeyCode.Space))
        {
            _holdTime = 0f;
            slider.value = _holdTime/timeForSkip;
        }
        
        if(!currentCutScene.skippable) return;
        
        if (!Input.GetKey(KeyCode.Space)) return;
        
        if (_isSkip) return;
        
        _holdTime += Time.deltaTime;
        slider.value = _holdTime/timeForSkip;
        
        if (!(_holdTime >= timeForSkip)) return;
        
        _isSkip = true;
        //StopCoroutine(PlayCutScene());
        if (currentCutScene.name is "Ending1" or "Ending2")
        {
            SceneManager.LoadScene("Main");
        }
        gameObject.SetActive(false);
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Debug/DebugEntry.cs
using System.Reflection;

public class DebugEntry
{
    public object Target;
    public FieldInfo Field;
    public MethodInfo Method;
    public string FieldName = "";

    public bool IsField => Field != null;
    public bool IsMethod => Method != null;
    
    public void SetFieldValue(string value)
    {
        var field = Field;

        object convertedValue = field.FieldType switch
        {
            var t when t == typeof(int) => int.Parse(value),
            var t when t == typeof(float) => float.Parse(value),
            var t when t == typeof(bool) => bool.Parse(value),
            _ => value
        };

        field.SetValue(Target, convertedValue);
    }

    public object GetValue()
    {
        if (IsField)
        {
            return Field.GetValue(Target);
        }

        if (IsMethod)
        {
            return Method.Name;
        }

        return null;
    }
    
    public void Execute()
    {
        Method.Invoke(Target, null);
    }
    
    public void Execute(string value)
    {
        ParameterInfo parameter =
            Method.GetParameters()[0];

        object convertedValue =
            parameter.ParameterType switch
            {
                var t when t == typeof(int)
                    => int.Parse(value),

                var t when t == typeof(float)
                    => float.Parse(value),

                var t when t == typeof(bool)
                    => bool.Parse(value),

                _ => value
            };

        Method.Invoke(
            Target,
            new[] { convertedValue });
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Debug/DebugInputPanel.cs
using System;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

public class DebugInputPanel : MonoBehaviour
{
    public TextMeshProUGUI fieldName;
    public TMP_InputField inputField;
    public DebugEntry DebugEntry;

    public void Init()
    {
        var str = "";
        if (DebugEntry.IsField)
        {
            str = $"{DebugEntry.Field.Name} : {DebugEntry.GetValue()}";
        } else if (DebugEntry.IsMethod)
        {
            str = $"{DebugEntry.Method.Name}";
            if(DebugEntry.Method.GetParameters().Length == 0) inputField.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
        if(DebugEntry.FieldName != null) str = DebugEntry.FieldName;
        fieldName.text = str;
    }
    
    public void OnButtonClick()
    {
        if(DebugEntry.IsField) DebugEntry.SetFieldValue(inputField.text);
        if (DebugEntry.IsMethod)
        {
            if(DebugEntry.Method.GetParameters().Length == 0) DebugEntry.Execute();
            else DebugEntry.Execute(inputField.text);
        }
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Debug/DebugLog.cs
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Text;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class DebugLog : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI log;
    [SerializeField] private ScrollRect scrollRect;
    
    private static DebugLog _instance;

    public static DebugLog GetInstance()
    {
        return _instance;
    }

    private void Init()
    {
        if (_instance == null)
        {
            _instance = this;
        }
        else
        {
            Destroy(gameObject);
        }
    }

    private void Awake()
    {
        Init();
        log.text = "";
    }

    public void Log(string text)
    {
        var stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
        stringBuilder.Append(log.text);
        var newLog = $"[{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss}] {text}";
        stringBuilder.Append(newLog+"\n");
        log.text = stringBuilder.ToString();
        Debug.Log(text);
        Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
        StartCoroutine(ScrollToBottom());
    }
    
    private IEnumerator ScrollToBottom()
    {
        Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
        yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.1f);

        Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();

        scrollRect.verticalNormalizedPosition = 0f;
    }
}

FILE Assets/Sanggu/Scripts/Debug/DebugMainPanel.cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class DebugMainPanel: MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private GameObject panel;
    [SerializeField] private GameObject logPanel;

    [SerializeField] private GameObject categoryPrefab;
    [SerializeField] private GameObject inputPanelPrefab;
    [SerializeField] private GameObject contents;
    
    public MonoBehaviour[] targets;
    
    private void OnEnable()
    {
        Init();
    }

    // F12 토글은 제거함. 테스트 단축키는 JSW/Script/DebugKeys.cs 에서 관리한다.

    private void Init()
    {
        var chlids = new List<GameObject>();
        foreach (Transform chlid in contents.transform)
        {
            chlids.Add(chlid.gameObject);
        }

        foreach (var chlid in chlids.ToList())
        {
            Destroy(chlid);
        }

        foreach (var target in targets)
        {
            var debugEntries = Collect(target);
            var category = Instantiate(categoryPrefab, contents.transform).GetComponent<DebugCategory>();
            category.categoryName.text = target.name;
            foreach (var debugEntry in debugEntries) 
            {
               var go = Instantiate(inputPanelPrefab, contents.transform);
               var input = go.GetComponent<DebugInputPanel>();
               category.contents.Add(go);
               input.DebugEntry = debugEntry;
               input.Init(); 
            }
        }
        
    }
    
    private List<DebugEntry> Collect(MonoBehaviour target)
    {
        List<DebugEntry> entries = new();
        //if(!target.didAwake) continue;
        var type = target.GetType();

        FieldInfo[] fields = type.GetFields(
            BindingFlags.Instance |
            BindingFlags.Public |
            BindingFlags.NonPublic);

        foreach (var field in fields)
        {
            if (field.GetCustomAttribute<DebugFieldAttribute>() != null)
            {
                entries.Add(new DebugEntry
                {
                    Target = target,
                    Field = field,
                    FieldName = field.GetCustomAttribute<DebugFieldAttribute>().FieldName,
                });
            }
        }

        MethodInfo[] methods = type.GetMethods(
            BindingFlags.Instance |
            BindingFlags.Public |
            BindingFlags.NonPublic);

        foreach (var method in methods)
        {
            if (method.GetCustomAttribute<DebugButtonAttribute>() != null)
            {
                entries.Add(new DebugEntry
                {
                    Target = target,
                    Method = method,
                    FieldName = method.GetCustomAttribute<DebugButtonAttribute>().MethodName
                });
            }
        }

        return entries;
    }
}
